### 263 subject events

- **1915.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Alois Lechner的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Alois Lechner把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Alois Lechner弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1918.03** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Klara扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Klara摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Klara一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Klara前面的大婶一直往后靠，Klara就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Klara的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.04** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-scarlet` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子里一家接一家地病倒。Klara烧到说胡话，Josef Bauer把阁楼小屋腾出来，在门把手上系了块红布。医生隔着门看了Klara的喉咙，留下药粉，收下一克朗二十赫勒。Josef Bauer把家里留着买煤的钱数了两遍，刚好够。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (付医生诊费); condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Moser从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Klara。Klara把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Luise Moser朝Klara招手。Klara挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Klara把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1923.07** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Klara蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Klara趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Katharina Bauer数完钱，脸色沉下来，Klara站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Luise Kral让Klara去区公所补。Klara跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Luise Kral看见Klara把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Klara赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Klara第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved_

- **1925.02** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-factory` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Katharina Bauer把Klara的书包从钉子上取下来，说厂里招装订工，明天就去。Grete Stein傍晚来家里，站在门口劝了半个钟头，Katharina Bauer只说灶上还坐着锅。第二天清早，Klara穿着改短的围裙出门，书包留在门后，里面还有半块橡皮。头一个月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_factory; role; education none_

- **1926.02** `r2-leisure.scullery-punishment::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/burned-palm` tags: domestic_service, abuse, punishment, burn, servant, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Leopoldstädter Volksschule抓住Klara的手腕，把掌心按在熨斗底座上。皮肉先发白，随后才红起来；Klara没有叫，只听见自己的呼吸。Josef Winter把一盆冷水端过来，Leopoldstädter Volksschule说不用，让她记住。
  _effects: mood -0.20000000000000004; condition; flag +burned_by_mistress; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; knowledge “the mistress burned a maid with an iron”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1926.05** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Klara把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Katharina Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Klara那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1927.01** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Klara推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Katharina Bauer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Klara在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Katharina Bauer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Klara在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Klara把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1927.05** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Anna Bauer把成绩单按在讲台上，说Klara还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Klara签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.10** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1927.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  介绍所里那个Friedrich Bauer说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Klara走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1937.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Josef Berg却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Klara把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Klara在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Emil Berg 2第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1938.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Luise Stein听，Luise Stein摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.court` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  兄弟把信推回来，说笔迹不对，办事处的人把清单合上，让两人去法院。Klara在楼下问过律师，诉讼费三十克朗。她把信按在胸口站了一会儿，楼梯间的灯一闪一闪，兄弟已经走到街对面。
  _effects: relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1938.06** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:failed-apprentice` tags: failed-apprentice, craft, work, failure, stigma, state, state_tick, state:failed-apprentice
  Klara的「学徒契约终止」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove failed-apprentice state:world-vienna-1912:114_

- **1939.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.sister` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  姐姐家在多瑙河对岸，院子里晾着床单。孩子在那里住了一个月，回来时脸圆了些，手里攥着一块河边的白石子。可他站在门口不肯叫Klara，姐姐蹲下来哄，Klara把行李提进屋，听见身后孩子叫了一声姨。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1939.02** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home
  Katharina Bauer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Klara晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱)_

- **1939.08** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Klara把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Klara拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1939.09** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-concert-ticket` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Klara不再让Friedrich Bauer传话，托其带去一张星期天音乐会的站票，花了一克朗二十赫勒，位置在柱子后头。票上不写多余的字，只说若不方便，票留着也无妨。Friedrich Bauer把票揣进衬衫内袋，Klara则把找回来的铜板一枚枚放进铁盒，等一个回音。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (音乐会站票一张（其中0.2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1939.10** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Klara在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Alois Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Alois Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Lena帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Klara点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org-wien-magistrat; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1940.02** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Klara把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Lena放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Luise Huber在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1940.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Franz Winter 2问是谁动的。Franz Winter 2说是Anna Stein嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1940.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Alois Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007881_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008237_

- **1941.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Klara把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Anna Huber在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Klara的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 名誉主席 @org-sdap; reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.07999999999999996; condition; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1941.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1941.05** `state_reaction:raising-child/child-death-in-the-household` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Klara没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 25; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 57; flag +lost_child_

  Klara不想打官司，把工具分成两堆，让Emil先挑。Emil挑走了刨子和凿子，留下那台旧车床；Klara把车床擦了一遍，发现底座下面压着父亲刻的一行小字：给肯留下来的那个。Anna Huber在旁边看着，没有说话。
  _effects: cash 2 (封蜡下面的名字); flag +split_the_fathers_tools; role; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “父亲在车床底下刻了一行字：给肯留下来的那个。”_

- **1941.07** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Franz Hofer在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Luise Kral没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:008259; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1941.08** `hist15-occ.seed-occupation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-flag-changes` tags: occupation, war, curfew, history, place, institution, vienna, urban
  城门口的旗换了颜色，岗哨开始检查每一张纸。Klara回家时发现街角新钉了一块告示牌：天黑以后不许出门，地窖里的粮食要登记。晚上，巡逻队的靴声从窗下经过，比钟声还准。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:243; flag +saw_the_occupation_flag_

  Klara的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Klara看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1943.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Luise Stein点了牛奶咖啡，Klara要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡（其中0.12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:300; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  Otto Berg回话说，对方家里已经应了另一门亲，只是还没下聘。Klara把准备好的小礼物——一块香皂和一双线手套——留在柜台上，没有托人送去；第二天上工时，Gustav Moser 2从对面走过，两人都装作在看橱窗。

  Klara把听来的话添了一句，讲给Luise Huber听。三天后，Alois Moser在集市上被人拦住问，回家把篮子摔在桌上。Klara说只是随口一提，Alois Moser说随口的话才最像真的。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999996; flag +spread_village_gossip; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002393:child_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.07999999999999996; position 名誉主席 @org-sdap_

  _effects: cash -4 (天花板上爬满了虫子（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition; role; knowledge “withdrawal can bring hallucinations and a fight against the ”_

  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  Emil Berg 2在档案柜最底层找到那张写错的卡片，用蓝笔划掉，重新抄了一遍。Klara在窗口等了四个钟头，交了两克朗抄录费，拿到一张盖了三个章的证明。Emil Berg 2说以后每年都要来核一次，不然系统还会把人写死。Klara把证明折好，出门时发现天已经黑了，路灯刚亮。
  _effects: cash -2 (死亡登记处的活人（其中0.25克朗记入债务）); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +spec14_record_corrected; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Klara坐在后厅角落的啤酒箱上听完整场排练，Gustav Hofer中途走过来问要不要在名册上签字，Klara说再想想。散场时人们三三两两去前厅续杯，Klara把空杯子放回柜台，顺着酒馆后门的巷子走回家，嘴里还留着半段旋律；这一晚没花钱，也和Gustav Hofer多说了两句话。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 58 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  疗养院的护士接过行李，把床号写在小黑板上。Klara躺下时听见走廊尽头有人在放唱片，咳嗽没那么紧了。安娜的信第三天到，说花浇过了，房东没来催租。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1948.10** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/quarrel-with-clerk` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Klara和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Klara在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.12** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Klara把照片铁盒交给Mitzi Lechner抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）); health -0.05 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1949.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011965_

- **1949.01** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/festival-king-only` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, vienna, urban
  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:373; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `r2-law.residency-permit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/permit-renewed` tags: law, residency, papers, police, housing, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Berg 2把居留证翻到住址页，说章盖的是旧地址，和申报的不一样。Klara解释上个月搬了家，Anton Weiss 2在门外探头，说合同是从月初算的。Emil Berg 2让Anton Weiss 2写一张确认书，又让Klara按手印；章盖下去时，正好压住了旧地址的半行字。
  _effects: cash -5 (居留章盖在旧地址上（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +residency_permit_renewed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; knowledge “a landlord’s confirmation can correct the address on a resid”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/mocked-by-younger-students` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara被年轻同学笑说口音重，念课文时全班哄笑。Klara把书合上，第二天没来。Grete Stein托人带话，Klara只说家里忙，其实每天那个时辰都坐在河边，用树枝在泥地上划自己的名字。河水涨起来的那天，Klara在泥地上写的名字被冲掉了，Klara又写了一遍。
  _effects: mood -0.09; skill_

- **1949.03** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012635_

- **1949.06** `r3-war.refugee-train::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::repatriation-train/met-returning-refugee-train` tags: war, refugee, train, station, migration, vienna, urban, postwar
  战后第三个月，Klara在站台上接一列从东边回来的车。Franz Winter从窗口探出头，瘦得只剩一双眼睛；Klara举着一块写错名字的纸牌，她看了半天才认出来。Emil Berg 2催着清场，Klara把纸牌塞进她手里，说“回家再说”。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +met_returning_refugee_train; role_

- **1949.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara先给Josef盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Josef吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1950.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/real-appointment` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Winter把一份任命书推到Klara面前。职位不大，但有印章、有预算、有能签字的地方。Klara签下名字时，第一次觉得那件一直想着的事有了一个可以摸到的边。
  _effects: reputation 0.12; position 部门主管 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:009275_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction

  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013127_

  开口时只提了手推车，钱的事咽回去。对方爽快地把车钥匙样的铁钩递过来，说用完了搁回院里就行。十克朗改去当铺想办法，一件旧外套和一只怀表抵了十二克朗，赎期三个月，票据折好收在证件夹里。
  _effects: cash 12 (当铺抵当旧外套和怀表换来的钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1950.04** `r3-crime.false-accusation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-housing/maid-confesses` tags: crime, court, servant, class, injustice, vienna, urban, postwar
  就在法官要敲槌时，Grete Huber忽然站起来，说糖罐是她打碎的，碎片埋在院子里的李树下。权力俱乐部的脸涨得通红，Berta Winter被叫去后院挖了半个钟头。Klara被当庭释放，门外下着雨，Grete Huber追出来，把一个纸包塞进Klara手里，里面是三个月的工钱。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; flag +cleared_by_maid_confession; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the sugar bowl was broken and buried in the yard”_

- **1950.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Hermann Moser没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1950.08** `kernel-social.neighbor-conflict::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::neighbor-conflict-scarcity/neighbor-conflict.ration-accusation` tags: social, neighbor, conflict, everyday, housing, scarcity, vienna, urban
  配给站说这个月每家少十公斤煤，邻居半夜把Klara家的煤筐挪到自己门后。Klara早上发现筐底只剩碎渣，站在走廊里问是谁拿的；没有人应，只有一扇门在里面上了闩。
  _effects: relation rival with person-marie-huber; role; cash -2 (replacing the coal taken from the basket（其中1.6克朗记入债务）); flag +ration_accusation; knowledge “这个月每家的煤少了十公斤，走廊里有人半夜挪煤筐。”_

- **1951.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara走到Versammlungsraum der graphischen Arbeiter门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Grete Weiss在午休时把本子摊在Klara的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Klara从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Grete Weiss当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004093:child; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1951.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Klara在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1951.09** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.06_

- **1952.01** `r15-wish.power-intrigue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/outmaneuvered` tags: politics, power, wish, intrigue, office, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Huber 2在会议上先一步拿到了多数。Klara的提案被改成一段附注，连名字都没有留下。散会后，Klara在走廊里站了一会儿，然后去下一间办公室。
  _effects: mood -0.060000000000000026; reputation -0.050000000000000044; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:438_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013127_

- **1952.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Berta Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Berta Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013708_

- **1952.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Anna Huber用Klara留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Klara发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Klara自己的名字。Anna Huber说只是替Klara省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.09999999999999998; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.07000000000000003_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014307_

- **1952.01** `r2-law.inheritance-tax-audit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/appealed-assessment` tags: law, inheritance, tax, audit, farm, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara对估价不服，找Elisabeth Moser写申诉，说母牛去年就死了。Ferdinand Huber 2要求死牛证明；兽医的证明在村里，村长说档案柜的钥匙在Ferdinand Huber 2手里。Klara跑了两趟，最后把申诉和一张村长签字的纸条一起递上去；Ferdinand Huber 2收下，说等三个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (遗产清单上的犁（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +appealed_inheritance_tax_assessment; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; knowledge “the village records are locked in the auditor’s own cabinet”_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Helene Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014470_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  星期天搬家的前一晚，有人坐在印厂友人家的厨房里把事说全：车要借，十克朗也要借。对方合上账本，从抽屉里数出两张五克朗，说月底先还三克朗，剩下的按月摊。手推车的轮轴有点响，搬之前得先上油。
  _effects: cash 10 (友人借给搬家应急的十克朗); mood 0.05_

  _effects: cash -1 (电车上的第三只手（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006873:child; knowledge “the boy returned the wallet but kept the coins”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

  报纸把Klara称作“新任主席”，照片很大，办公室很漂亮。真正的决定在另一间屋里做出。Klara每天签字，签的都是别人写好的东西。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994; reputation 0.07999999999999996; position 名誉主席 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:008259_

- **1953.01** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Berta 2排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Klara，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Grete Lechner这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 43 -> 38; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 44 -> 49; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.03_

  Klara在回家路上被拦住，问话从晚上八点持续到凌晨。没有正式指控，但名字被写进了另一本册子。
  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.3 -> 0.44999999999999996; mood -0.08_

- **1953.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/collected-forty-kroner` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Luise Kral把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Klara把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person-luise-kral; organization_funds; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

- **1953.05** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/reappointed` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  计票拖到凌晨，Klara以两票之差留任。Anna Huber把已经写好的交接清单折起来，笑着说下次再来。Klara回到办公室，发现桌上的文件被人重新排过；最上面那份，是新一年的预算草案。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; mood 0.06; organization_funds; state_add office-patronage state:world-vienna-1912:462; flag +reappointed; knowledge “桌上的文件被人重新排过，最上面是新一年的预算草案。”; flag +office_patronage_network_

- **1954.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Emil Bauer在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Anna Huber没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.06000000000000005; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:015532_

  _effects: cash -1 (两床之间的一把椅子); mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +small_marriage_repair_

  _effects: cash -20 (三楼走廊里的煤油味); death (smoke inhalation in a tenement fire); flag -office_patronage_network_

- **1914.06** `vienna-infant-household.cradle-fall/neighbor-warns` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  邻居上楼借盐，看见摇床缺口，顺手用自己篮子里一块旧布垫在床沿，又叮嘱Alois Lechner把床挪离窗边冷墙。Mitzi这次翻身被布兜住，Alois Lechner第二天下工把床挪了半米。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; health 0.020000000000000018 (摇床被加固，减少了磕碰); mood 0.03_

- **1917.05** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Mitzi踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Alois Lechner把Mitzi抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Mitzi举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Mitzi的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Mitzi在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Hermann Lechner一挣，Mitzi只能把Hermann Lechner抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Mitzi背着Hermann Lechner在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Hermann Lechner擦鼻涕。等Hermann Lechner睡沉，Mitzi的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Mitzi前面的大婶一直往后靠，Mitzi就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Mitzi的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1921.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Mitzi跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Mitzi从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Gustav Berg喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Mitzi手心。Mitzi低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Gustav Berg扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Otto Winter在绳子那头喊。Mitzi踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Otto Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Mitzi把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Mitzi后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Mitzi没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Mitzi用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1922.06** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Mitzi蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Mitzi趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Alois Lechner数完钱，脸色沉下来，Mitzi站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06_

  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Mitzi站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Grete Winter在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Mitzi掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Mitzi才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开)_

  书在Mitzi的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Mitzi把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Ferdinand Weiss 2翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Mitzi。

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Mitzi分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Mitzi的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; health -0.040000000000000036 (standing all day); condition_

  客户把床单贴在脸上，又摸了摸边角，说“还是潮的”，把门合上一半。Mitzi把床单卷回筐里，背回洗衣房。Grete Bauer 2没骂，只让在后院重晾，件钱等重送完再结。Mitzi蹲在地上翻布角，晚风吹得膝盖发酸，天黑前才夹完最后一只夹子。

- **1926.04** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Mitzi的手，又让Mitzi推两下刨子试试。Mitzi用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Rosa Lechner，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。

  Anton Lechner把Mitzi的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Mitzi。Mitzi进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。

  Gustav Berg把表格推回来，说Mitzi的住址不在招工范围。Mitzi问哪里还在招人，对方已经低头叫下一位。回家路上，Mitzi看见修配厂门口排着另一条队，队尾的人比早上更多。
  _effects: unemployment (没有劳动通行证); flag +permit_refused_

- **1939.01** `r13-work.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/joint-fits` tags: work, craft, practice, wood, skill, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi把第四十个榫头敲进卯眼，声音是闷的，不晃。Anton Weiss路过，用拇指按了按接缝，说这回不用胶也能站住。Mitzi把废掉的三十九个榫头扫进灶膛，火苗从锯末里窜起来，屋里全是松木的味。
  _effects: cash 2 (第四十个榫头); flag +work13_forty_practice_mortises; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1939.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工资袋上的油墨还没干透，Mitzi的手指染了一点黑。母亲把硬币倒在厨房桌上，一枚枚排开，排到第六枚时停住。灶上的水壶响了，她拿起来又放下，没继续问。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

  Alois Lechner把Mitzi寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Mitzi追到码头时，Alois Lechner已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Mitzi在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-alois-lechner; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1940.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.sister` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  嫁妆单念完，Alois Lechner发现少写了一对银勺，那是母亲留下的。Grete Stein说银勺早该算在聘礼里，两家各执一词，茶凉了也没人喝。Mitzi走进去，把银勺放进Grete Stein手里，说这样就不用再争了；屋里静得能听见勺子碰碗沿。
  _effects: cash -4 (嫁妆单上的最后一行（其中4克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.039999999999999994; flag +dowry_spoons_given_away; role; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1940.11** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anton Weiss的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1940.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi没有立刻答话，先去敲Grete Huber的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Grete Huber提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Mitzi把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1941.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Anton Weiss在几天后找到Mitzi，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; household; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007824_

- **1941.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/soup-kitchen-opened` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi把工会活动室的长桌拼起来，用两口大锅煮扁豆汤。Gustav Lechner赊了半袋豆子和一筐洋葱，条件是罢工结束后先还他。第一天来了三十七个人，Mitzi给每个碗里多舀半勺；汤不够时，就往锅里再添一壶水。
  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅（其中1克朗记入债务）); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; organization_funds_

- **1941.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Berta Moser说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Mitzi站在原地看着Berta Moser拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001470:child; flag +uncertain_courtship_

  Mitzi没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Mitzi去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000272:child_

  Anna Huber把征用单按在柜台上，Mitzi家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Anna Huber说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1942.02** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-list-was-already-signed` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  上一任在离任前签了一份四十人的任命名单，最后一行没有日期。Mitzi把Friedrich Winter 2的名字添在第四十一行，注明“补录”；发薪日，会计问这四十一个人里为什么有六个已经去世。
  _effects: position 编外顾问 @workshop:person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006864; flag +forty_first_name; knowledge “上一任留下的四十人名单里，有六个已经去世。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1942.02** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Emil Hofer 2在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1942.04** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.school-fork/ask-for-reduction` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Mitzi带着成绩单去学校找Grete Stein，问减免和奖学金。Grete Stein领到办公室，一位Luise Kral翻着册子说可以减一半书本费，服装费仍得自己出，答复要等开学前一周。Mitzi把两张纸重新折好，回家没有对母亲提准信，只把桌布底下的决定又压了几天。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +awaiting_school_aid_

  _effects: cash -2 (coal tickets set aside for the neighbour nurse); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; role; flag +nurse_paid_in_bread_

  _effects: cash 2 (把铺盖搬上三楼); role; relation colleague with person-mitzi-lechner; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; household; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  Mitzi把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Mitzi自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1944.01** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000272:child_

  Mitzi输了五票，要求把票重新数一遍。数到第二遍还是五票，Hermann Weiss 2把铁盒盖上，说“再数就难看了”。Mitzi说账本也得查，后厅一下子安静下来。那晚的合唱没有唱成。散会后，Hermann Weiss 2把铁盒抱在怀里，锁进酒馆老板的柜子。铁盒被放回柜子，账本却留在了桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; reputation -0.05; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1944.02** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  她付钱时，摊主找了几个铜板，顺手把杯子用旧报纸裹上。回家拆开报纸，杯底那道裂纹在灯下看得更清楚。她还是倒了半杯茶进去试，看水从缝里渗出来，在桌上慢慢汪成一小片。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买下有裂纹的描金边杯子); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1944.08** `r1-cul.occult-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/white-shape-explained` tags: faith, rumour, occult, neighbourhood, scapegoat, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi在井边看见一个白影，第二天整条街都在传。Luise Huber说那是洗衣妇的床单，Anton说井里死过人，不该在夜里靠近。Mitzi半夜提灯去看，白影又出现了，是一条挂在树枝上的衬裙。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +investigated_well_ghost; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; knowledge “the white shape is a petticoat on the plum tree”_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Mitzi在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine（其中4克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1945.01** `r13-edu.study-group::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/both-pass-exam` tags: education, study_group, mutual_aid, politics, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi和Franz Winter 2在阁楼里支起一块旧门板当桌子，每周三晚上互相考问。Franz Winter 2把容易错的公式写在烟盒背面，Mitzi负责讲解历史背景。考试结果出来，两个人都过了，Franz Winter 2把烟盒送给Mitzi作纪念，说以后别再弄丢。烟盒被Mitzi放在书架上，一直没拆。
  _effects: flag +study_group_passed; skill_

- **1945.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Friedrich Bauer合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1945.11** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-refused` tags: faith, social, family, home
  登记员翻遍册子，说这户上个月已经领过。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。

- **1946.01** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.kahlenberg-sunday/kahlenberg-rain` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  走到半山就落雨，三人挤进酒农的棚子，把篮子里的面包和盐分完，吃食折合一克朗。Friedrich Bauer把外套顶在头上先跑，Katharina Weiss与Mitzi留在最后收篮子；雨点打在葡萄叶上，Mitzi的鞋袜湿到傍晚，脚趾发麻，谁也没急着走。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000431:child_

- **1946.01** `r14-soc.confront-rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/made-it-worse` tags: task, rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, confrontation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi在院子里把Mitzi Lechner堵住，声音大得三层楼都听见。Mitzi Lechner索性把话又说了一遍，还添了两个细节。围观的人越来越多，Mitzi最后把手里的一篮衣服摔在地上，转身回屋。第二天，Mitzi Lechner把晾衣绳往自家窗边挪了半尺，Mitzi没有作声。那篮衣服在院子里躺到第二天，被雨淋透了。
  _effects: reputation -0.15; condition; flag +escalated_rumor; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1946.02** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Mitzi把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Gustav Winter替Mitzi扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002385:child_

- **1946.05** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-scavenge-station` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
  _effects: food_

- **1946.10** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Karl Kraus抱着孩子连声道谢时，Mitzi已经弯着腰在咳。药房玻璃瓶上贴着褪色标签，喝下去满嘴甘草味；后半夜Mitzi听见自己牙齿打颤，炉子早灭了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药); relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus_

- **1947.01** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi和Anna Stein一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Mitzi去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Mitzi先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1947.01** `r13-fam.seek-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rebuffed` tags: task, social, friendship, loneliness, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi刚开口，Klara就把报纸举高，说这张桌子有人。Mitzi端着杯子在门口站了一会儿，回去把椅子放正；第二天同一时辰，Klara没有来，桌上留着一圈没擦干的杯印。
  _effects: flag +rebuffed_while_seeking_friend; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1947.06** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铜板一枚枚落在桌面上，比说好的少了十五赫勒。Mitzi没去捡，先问Franz Winter 2下捆还送不送。Franz Winter 2含糊应了一声，拎起布包就走。Otto Huber这才进来，说下回得先讲好价。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1947.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi把通知念给Friedrich Bauer听，Friedrich Bauer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Mitzi去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food_

- **1947.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Emil Berg 2在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Mitzi把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Mitzi一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Luise Moser 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `kernel-work.task-household-debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/work-debt-new-loan` tags: task, work, debt, household, credit, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi还不上旧账，只好向Marie Huber介绍的放贷人再借十克朗，月息两成。Hermann Weiss 2收了旧账，又重新开了一页赊账；Mitzi在两本账上都按了手印。回家路上，Mitzi把两张借据分开藏，怕家里人看见。
  _effects: cash 10 (借新债还旧账); flag +new_debt; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; knowledge “放贷人的月息是两成，借据要分开藏。”; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1948.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Alois Moser拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002393:child_

- **1948.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Katharina Weiss把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Katharina Weiss在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000431:child; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1948.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi上楼敲了Alois Moser的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Alois Moser把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Mitzi能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002393:child; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1949.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Moser嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Alois Moser家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Alois Moser那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002393:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011972_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.join-club::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/voice-rejected` tags: task, social, club, choir, organization, belonging, vienna, urban
  Mitzi唱到第二段就被Friedrich Bauer打断，说声音太直，会把整排带跑。Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的人没有笑，只是把歌本收回去。Mitzi在门口等到散场，问能不能先搬椅子、烧炉子；Friedrich Bauer说缺的是嗓子，不是手。
  _effects: mood -0.09000000000000008; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; knowledge “the choir chooses by voice, not by willingness”_

- **1949.02** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.wring-blanket` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  两个人拧毯子的时候，Rosa Berg 2在旁边递肥皂水，说你们俩力气倒合得上。毯子抬上绳子时还在滴水，Mitzi甩了甩手，看见自己掌纹里嵌着一道一道的布纹。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1949.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Anton Moser在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Mitzi问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000148:child; skill_

- **1949.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.together-to-office` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi和Grete Huber叫上楼下两家，一同去房东的事务所。Ferdinand Huber 2隔着柜台听他们说完，翻出账簿，答应先补上楼梯间的窗闩，涨租从下下个月起算。四人出门时挤在台阶上，谁也没先说话，Grete Huber把帽子往后一推，说总算没白跑。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.district-office-queue` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  区公所的长凳坐满了人。Ferdinand Huber 2的笔尖在名册上划了一道，把纸推过来时没有抬眼。Mitzi把纸折了三折，塞进Anton手里。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `kernel-civic.denunciation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-denunciation-informer` tags: politics, denunciation, repression, neighbor, war, vienna, urban, postwar
  她为了把自己从名单上摘出来，写下了隔壁男人的名字。Ferdinand Huber 2把纸收进公文包，说“早这样就好了”；她回家时，隔壁的门已经锁上，煤箱里只剩一层灰。第二天，警察在隔壁门口贴了一张封条，浆糊还没干。
  _effects: flag +denounced_someone; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; condition; cash 2 (配合调查后领到的补助); knowledge “告发者的名字不会写进邻居的案卷，但警察会记得是谁递的纸”_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Mitzi和Friedrich Winter按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Mitzi喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1951.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-benefit-office/sent-to-relief-work` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Winter说补助金只发给有家属的人，Mitzi单身，只能去修路队领工钱。第二天Mitzi扛着铁锹站在城郊，和几十个男人一起把碎砖填进车辙。工钱按日结，比补助多两克朗，但一天下来，手掌磨出了三个水泡。
  _effects: cash 2 (补助金窗口的第二张表); condition; flag +work13_sent_to_relief_work_

- **1951.07** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  有人喊罢工，有人喊谈判，Mitzi站到前排说先动才有得谈。保守派转身去拿大衣，Berta Moser追出去又回来，说外头雪深，别把人推到对面去。会议没散，火气没降。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001470:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.09** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Emil Bauer借给Mitzi一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Mitzi睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1952.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Mitzi和Mitzi Moser合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001905:child; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1952.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Mitzi把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.arrange-move::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/trunk-withheld` tags: social, housing, moving, landlord, cash, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把最后一箱书搬下楼时，Anton Weiss 2堵在门口，说墙皮被蹭掉一块，箱子先留下。Mitzi说要赶车，Anton Weiss 2说那就把修墙的钱付了。板车在巷口按铃，Mitzi只抱走了装证件的木盒。
  _effects: cash -2 (板车与押金收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +landlord_withheld_trunk; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; knowledge “the landlord keeps the trunk against a wall repair claim”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1952.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.two-slices-of-bread` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi说自家也快见底了，不能再借面粉，临关门前往Ferdinand Bauer手里塞了两块面包，说先给孩子垫一垫。Ferdinand Bauer攥着面包站了一会儿，说了声谢谢就走了，楼梯上传来脚步和纸袋的磨蹭声。
  _effects: food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Mitzi缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1953.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Mitzi的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price（其中7.5克朗记入债务）); health 0.04000000000000001 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1953.09** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  介绍所里那个Anton说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Mitzi走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.flea-market-cup/flea-market-cup.haggle-cheap` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, flea_market, object
  摊主是个戴眼镜的老头，开价时把杯子往摊布上一顿。Mitzi指出杯口有个磕痕，Gustav Moser又添了一句裂纹渗水。老头摘下眼镜擦了擦，说三十就三十，反正搁着也是搁着。她数出三枚十赫勒的硬币，心里算着比杂货铺的新杯子还是便宜。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); skill_

- **1953.12** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Mitzi把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Klara替Mitzi扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1954.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Karl Kraus从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-karl-kraus_

- **1954.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.look-for-cheaper-bed` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Mitzi去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1954.10** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-credit-full` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居帮你把半筐煤抬上楼，楼梯窄，煤块滚了两级。你蹲下去捡，指甲缝里立刻嵌了黑泥。炉子生起来后，屋里那股潮冷气慢慢退了，你才想起晚饭还没着落。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (赊煤到期还款含利息（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); food; flag +coal_credit_debt_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi看Ferdinand Berg盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Ferdinand Berg愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Mitzi门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Mitzi打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015539_

- **1955.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015539_

- **1955.01** `r13-work.ask-foreman::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/given-broom-and-number` tags: work, hiring, foreman, favour, queue, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Weiss 2正蹲在机器边上换皮带，听完没抬头，只把粉笔从耳朵后面取下来，在门框上写了个“七”。他说七点前来，先扫地、添油，工钱按杂工算。Mitzi把那个数字记在手心，回家路上一直攥着拳头，怕汗把它抹掉。
  _effects: cash 1 (工头兜里的粉笔头); flag +work13_foreman_gave_a_start; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; employment Hilfsarbeiter @ org-winter-print_

- **1955.04** `r2-leisure.gramophone::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/repaired-the-needle` tags: leisure, gramophone, music, etiquette, accident, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把唱针抬起来，对着灯看针尖，发现上面挂着一小团绒线。Alois Bauer教过，用火柴盒侧面刮一刮就行。Mitzi刮完，把唱片重新放上，同一句唱到第三遍时，Berta Moser终于笑了。Katharina Winter 2把酒杯放下，说这比新唱片还好听。
  _effects: flag +repaired_gramophone_needle; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001470:child; knowledge “a needle with lint will skip on the same groove”_

- **1955.05** `r1-work.pawnshop-watch::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/let-the-watch-go` tags: commerce, pawnshop, debt, family, theft, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Kral把到期牌翻成红色，说再不来就归铺子。Mitzi摸了摸空口袋，说那就归吧。走出门时，她听见柜台里面有人给表上发条，声音很轻，像谁在咳嗽，又像父亲在楼上翻报纸。
  _effects: cash 6 (柜台上的银怀表); mood -0.08000000000000007; flag +sold_family_watch_

- **1955.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Friedrich Weiss 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Friedrich Weiss 2敲开Mitzi的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `r13-fam.mediate-sibling-conflict::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::sibling-postwar/soldier-brother-leaves` tags: task, family, sibling, conflict, mediation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Lechner说在这个家里住不下去，卷起军毯就要走。Hermann Lechner没有拦，只把门打开。Mitzi追到楼梯口，把母亲留下的那只汤勺塞进Mitzi Lechner的背包，说走到哪儿都要吃饭。
  _effects: condition; flag +soldier_sibling_left_home_

- **1956.03** `r3-war.armistice-eleven::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::first-armistice-day/told-armistice-story` tags: war, armistice, ceasefire, aftermath, memory, vienna, urban, postwar
  纪念仪式上，Mitzi Moser问Mitzi当年停战时在做什么。Mitzi说在擦枪，擦到一半听见外面安静了，还以为是耳朵坏了。Mitzi Moser笑，Mitzi也笑，笑完把手里那支没点的烟放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +told_armistice_story_to_neighbor; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001905:child_

- **1956.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.repeat-at-bakery` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi在面包店排队时把听来的话又讲了一遍，添了“夜里常有男人上楼”一句。到傍晚，整栋楼都在说二楼女房客的事，连看门人都在门口站着看。二楼的门一直关着，Mitzi回家时才觉得那句话说得太响。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148_

- **1956.10** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Mitzi在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Elisabeth Huber 2的门口。傍晚Elisabeth Huber 2来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1956.11** `fa17-studio.private-failure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-as-pulp` tags: art, writing, failure, poverty, privacy, craft, plagiarism, vienna
  Mitzi把没有写完的稿子卖给一家专门印消遣读物的小出版社，换来的钱刚好够付一个月房租。编辑把结尾改成了火车相撞，又在封面上印了一个陌生的笔名。Mitzi在报摊上看见那本册子时，先认出了自己写的一句雨，然后才认出那个名字不是自己的。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉残稿的稿费); state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:533; reputation 0.009999999999999995; knowledge “出版社把结尾改成了火车相撞，封面上的笔名不是我。”; flag +sold_under_pseudonym_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/another-rejection` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  画店老板把Mitzi的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Mitzi抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 40 -> 47; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 3 -> 2; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 35 -> 32; health -0.020000000000000018 (冬天阁楼里没有足够的煤)_

- **1957.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.negotiate-hours/negotiate-hours.half-day` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, hours, saturday
  Anton Bauer 2在旁边悄悄比了个手势，让他别逼太紧。东家最后说中午收摊，算一天的钱扣两克朗。Mitzi点头，把自己那块怀表跟店里的钟对了一眼。
  _effects: cash -2 (星期六下午工钱按日扣减); health 0.030000000000000027 (终于有半天空闲); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-173_

- **1957.08** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Mitzi的裙角。Helene Bauer的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Mitzi咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004837:child; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1957.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Mitzi Moser把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Mitzi说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Helene Bauer在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Mitzi的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001905:child; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004837:child_

- **1958.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Mitzi没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017713_

  巡警搜了地下室，印刷机被抬上卡车。Gustav Hofer从后窗逃走，她留在门口，说自己是来收房租的；Franz Kral 2翻了她的包，只找到一把菜刀和半块面包。她看着印刷机被抬上车，想起自己第一次摇那台机器。
  _effects: flag +press_seized; condition; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-181; cash -8 (被搜走的印刷机和纸张损失); unemployment (地下印刷所被搜后失去工作); knowledge “巡警搜地下室时会先抬机器，后窗是唯一出口”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-181_

- **1958.07** `r3-ind.scab-labor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-to-work-beside-him` tags: work, strike, scab, ostracism, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把工具箱搬到车间另一头，说不同工贼共用一台砂轮。Gustav Moser来劝，说人已经进了厂，赶不走。Helene Bauer站在原来的工位上，手里没有活，工牌是新的，绳子还发白。Anton Winter 4最后把Mitzi调去仓库，工钱少了，但不再看见那张脸。
  _effects: cash -2 (复工第一天的同一排机器); flag +refused_to_work_beside_a_scab; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004837:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; knowledge “the new man kept the old striker’s machine and a new badge”_

- **1958.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  抽屉一推就卡住，Mitzi用膝盖顶了一下才合严。上工路上经过药房，橱窗里摆着润肺糖浆的广告画，她别过头去。车间主任点名时，她应声比平时慢了一拍。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (肺病未治继续劳累); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1958.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Josef合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1958.12** `r1-cul.radio-broadcast::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/spoke-the-cut-line` tags: culture, radio, broadcast, voice, censorship, vienna, urban, postwar
  直播前十分钟，Emil Winter把稿子里的一段删了，说上面打过招呼。Mitzi对着麦克风念到缺口处，停了一拍，把被删的那句用自己的话说了出来。Katharina Winter 3的手悬在切断键上，最终没有按下去。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +spoke_uncensored_line; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005899:child; knowledge “the station receives a list of lines to cut before each broa”_

- **1959.01** `r3-crime.stop-and-search::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-the-search` tags: crime, police, papers, street, humiliation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把口袋按住，说要看搜查令。Anton Winter笑了一声，反手把她的胳膊别到背后。Anton Bauer站在面包店门口，没有过来。到了岗亭，登记簿上写的不是“拒搜”，而是“妨碍公务”，Mitzi的胳膊肿了三天。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (路灯下翻出的口袋内衬); condition; flag +resisted_stop_and_search; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000276:child; knowledge “the register recorded resistance, not refusal”_

  Mitzi把旧练习本翻到空白一页，写下母亲、自己和Rosa的名字。十四克朗的药费单，Mitzi和Rosa各摊五克朗，余下四克朗从母亲的养老金里扣；Mitzi把五克朗硬币一枚枚摞在桌沿，Rosa盯着那一栏看了半天，也从围裙口袋里摸出钱来。
  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 45 -> 49; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 2 -> 5; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 47 -> 45; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中7克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (住院处窗口的押金); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

  她本来只是去面包店买盐，被琴声绊住了脚。猴子蹲在帽沿上啃一颗栗子，风琴师脱下礼帽扇风，露出汗湿的额角。曲子完了，她数出两枚铜板放进帽子，听见身后有人吹口哨。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  Josef交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  墙上出现了一行关于Josef的脏话，Katharina Berg让全班提水刷墙，刷了三遍还能看见白印。Lena在背后学那句话的腔调，Josef回头时，他正低头抠桌角的漆。
  _effects: reputation -0.060000000000000005; condition; flag +target_of_sexual_rumor; relation rival with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

  Josef把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Josef折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.04999999999999999; skill_

- **1930.02** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Friedrich Weiss 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Weiss 2在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; mood 0.03999999999999998; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1930.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Karl Kraus把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-karl-kraus_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.reference/p9-work-career.reference.strong` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, reference, certificate
  Josef把新东家的信放在柜台上。Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien读完，抽出公司的信笺，蘸墨水写下“勤勉可靠，品行端正，因故离柜，并无纠葛”。写完吹干，盖了章，还多写一句“有需要可来函”。Josef把信纸夹进厚纸板，第二天一早就往玛丽亚希尔夫的新账房去了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +has_strong_reference_

- **1931.06** `p10-work-career.first-position/p10-work-career.first-position.daily` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, first_job
  旧东家扣着学徒证明不放手，厂里只肯按日雇Josef。Anna Huber帮他跟门房说了几句，他每天天亮进厂，干到汽笛响第二遍，日结一克朗二十赫勒。Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien说干满一个月再看名册上有没有位置。
  _effects: employment 机器厂日工 @ org-wien-magistrat; flag +awaiting_workbook; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Friedrich Weiss 2提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  _effects: flag +friendship_at_the_water_tap; role; skill; relation friend with person-marie-huber_

  书箱落地时扬起一层灰，Mitzi Lechner蹲下把绳子解开，说这些书比家具还金贵。Josef靠着墙喘气，Gustav Moser 2在边上笑，说下回这家的活儿还来。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (搬书箱额外给的辛苦钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1947.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Marie Huber夹着烟走到门口。Josef报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1947.11** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-go-downstairs` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  听了那些话，Josef坐不住，当晚走到Katharina Winter 2家楼下，请看门人把原信送上去，自己站在门洞里等。看门人下来时手里拿着那封信，信封还是拆开的，说楼上让有话当面讲，不隔着人传。Josef把帽子捏来捏去，与Helene Weiss之间也添了一层不自在。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Josef Winter在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Josef Winter在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003650:child; mood 0.03999999999999998; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）)_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/loan-with-schedule` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Grete Huber听Josef把房租和药费的数目报完，从抽屉里数出二十克朗，推到桌上，同时拿出一张纸，要写还款日期。他说每月十五号来一趟，把账报一遍。Josef签字时手有点抖，把纸角压得很平，说好先从月底还三克朗。
  _effects: cash 20 (舅舅借出的钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +owes_uncle_

- **1948.03** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1949.01** `r13-civ.ration-card::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ration-card-issued` tags: politics, rationing, food, war, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Winter把配给证从本子上撕下来，填上Josef家的门牌和人数，又用打孔机在角上打了一个洞。Josef把证举到灯下，看见上面印着面粉、糖和煤的数量，每一项都比去年少。
  _effects: food; flag +ration_card_issued; knowledge “the monthly ration is smaller than last year’s”_

  Ferdinand Huber 2说今天的号发完了，让Josef明天再来。Josef问能不能在走廊里等到天亮，Ferdinand Huber 2说这里不许过夜。Josef把湿行李拖到街边，坐在门口的石阶上，看见里面的人一队队领到热汤。Ferdinand Huber 2关窗时，把百叶帘拉了下来。
  _effects: cash -3 (登记处门外的湿行李（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +refused_refugee_registration; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; knowledge “the registration office stops issuing numbers before noon”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145_

  排练到一半，有人提出只唱德语，另一边的成员把歌本合上。Grete Stein敲了敲谱架，说教堂不是议会。Katharina Winter站起来走了，Josef坐在原位，听见门在身后关上。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +choir_split_over_language; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001430:child; knowledge “half the choir wants German only and the other half refuses”_

- **1950.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef数出八赫勒交给Maria Weiss 2，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Maria Weiss 2说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Josef盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1950.07** `r1-hc.manslaughter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/flees-the-stairs` tags: violence, death, police, guilt, stairs, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef跑回家，把沾了灰的外套塞进灶膛。火苗起来时，Katharina Winter在外头敲门，问刚才楼梯响什么；Josef隔着门说，猫。那天夜里，Josef把炉子烧得很旺，屋里热得坐不住。
  _effects: mood -0.4; condition; flag +fled_fatal_fall; role; knowledge “the coat was burned before the police were called”_

- **1950.10** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +wish:craft:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013049_

  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Mitzi送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1951.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/childcare-arranged` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  Josef天没亮就把Franz 2裹好，抱到楼下Luise Kral家。Luise Kral接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Josef看灶上温着的米汤。Josef把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Luise Kral数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Josef听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

- **1951.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Josef把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002669:child; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

- **1951.11** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Katharina Weiss谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Katharina Weiss说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Rosa Moser听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000431:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012022_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Grete Huber提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1952.05** `r2-leisure.club-blackball::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::club-blackball-postwar/disputed-blackball-rule` tags: leisure, club, membership, class, exclusion, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef的提名被搁置了两次，第三次终于投票。黑球只有两颗，但Anna Huber说按章程，两颗也算否决。Marie Huber翻开章程，说章程写的是三颗。Anton Hofer 2在旁边低声说，章程去年改过。Josef把帽子拿在手里，等他们吵完。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +disputed_club_blackball_rule; role; relation friend with person-marie-huber; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; organization_membership; knowledge “the club rulebook was amended last year without a vote”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1952.11** `r2-uni.readers-letters::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/verified-the-letter` tags: media, newspaper, letters, verification, reputation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi来信说邻居家的儿子“靠关系进了邮政局”，要求登报。Josef去问了邮政局的录事，又找到考试名单，发现那年轻人是考了第三名。Josef把信退回去，附了一句“下次先查名单”。Mitzi回信只有两个字：“谢谢。”
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +verified_readers_letter; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the postal clerk was hired by examination, not favour”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_
