  Josef替Grete Huber写信，把最后知道的部队番号、收容站和一处旧住址都写上去。Grete Huber不识字，却要Josef把“家里都好”念三遍，怕写错。信投进邮筒时，她伸手在筒口摸了一下；Josef把回信地址又描粗了一笔。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”_

- **1953.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Josef当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004837:child_

- **1953.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Gustav Moser一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Gustav Moser的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1953.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1954.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Moser嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Gustav Moser家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Gustav Moser那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1954.01** `r13-work.ask-foreman::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/nephew-got-the-place` tags: work, hiring, foreman, favour, queue, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Huber 2的侄子从后面进来，手里提着两瓶酒。Ferdinand Huber 2把酒塞到柜台底下，转头对Josef说位子满了。Josef在门口站了一会儿，听见里面有人笑，笑声和皮带轮的响声混在一起。回家的路上，Josef第一次绕到工会办事处的窗前。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +work13_foreman_nephew_got_the_job; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; knowledge “the foreman reserves places for his relatives”_

- **1954.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Mitzi Moser用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001905:child; flag +parish_registered_

- **1955.01** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-scavenge-station` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef在柜台前把两枚硬币捏出汗，才开口请Ferdinand Kral喝一杯。Ferdinand Kral挑了靠窗的小桌，把糖块推给Josef，说这里比家里暖和。两人坐到灯亮，杯底的渣子凉透了，Josef还是没有把那句“以后常来”说出口。
  _effects: cash -2 (靠窗那张小桌); mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +bought_a_friend_coffee; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006421:child_

- **1955.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Josef没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

  战前那家鞋铺没有撑到战后，货架还钉在墙上。Gustav Hofer把租金压到战前的七成，只要Josef肯自己修门面。Josef用旧报纸糊了墙，把“鞋”字改成“杂货”，开张那天，整条街的人都来看新招牌。
  _effects: cash -9 (卷帘门上的第一块木牌); flag +work13_took_a_postwar_vacant_shop; role; employment Kaufmann @ org-die-fackel_

- **1956.03** `r3-crime.poisoning-case::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::one-microscope/drank-from-the-pot` tags: crime, medical, poison, marriage, suspicion, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef先盛了一碗，说“我先尝咸淡”。半个钟头后，Josef开始呕吐，Katharina Winter 2跑去叫Katharina Stein 3。洗胃时，Josef听见Berta Moser在门外问“谁做的汤”，Katharina Winter 2说“是他自己”。Katharina Stein 3把铜勺放进证物袋，说“先查查锅”。
  _effects: cash -6 (汤锅边搁着一把铜勺); condition; flag +poisoned_by_own_soup; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; knowledge “the soup was poisoned before the subject tasted it”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

  Josef把一整袋面粉倒进Alois Winter的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Alois Winter连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Josef当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1956.07** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-queue-clash` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Josef怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Alois Bauer一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.12** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +watch_pawned_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: death (暴力)_

- **1913.09** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-enough` tags: infancy, food, household
  Rosa Lechner在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg给Rosa喂奶，随后把洗净的布尿片挂在窗边。孩子吃饱后睡着了，锅里还留着半碗燕麦糊。
  _effects: cash -2 (milk and oatmeal)_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Rosa正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Hermann Lechner挡穿堂风。Hermann Lechner在旧布底下翻了个身，Rosa立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Alois Lechner推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Hermann Lechner，再看见Rosa冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1918.04** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1918.05** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Rosa Lechner把Rosa放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Rosa领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Rosa抓着Rosa Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.07999999999999996 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1918.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1918.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1918.08** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Otto Winter的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Rosa坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Otto Winter把桶一放，夹起Rosa进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Alois Lechner赶来时，Rosa正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Rosa的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Rosa在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Hermann Lechner一挣，Rosa只能把Hermann Lechner抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Rosa背着Hermann Lechner在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Hermann Lechner擦鼻涕。等Hermann Lechner睡沉，Rosa的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work

  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

  Hermann Lechner哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Rosa蹲下把Hermann Lechner驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Hermann Lechner听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Rosa的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Rosa Lechner回来接过孩子，Rosa扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。

- **1922.05** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home

  Friedrich Lechner让Rosa放学后送两趟货，按趟给钱，天黑前必须回铺子交账。Rosa把书包挂在柜台后面，提着篮子穿过三条街；第一趟送错了一户，第二趟跑得鞋底发热。收工时Friedrich Lechner数了三个铜板，说明天还来。

  团里有人偷了市场的香肠，被抓住后供出了所有人。Mitzi把Rosa拉到楼梯拐角，让她把责任推给最小的那个，说这样大家都能脱身。Rosa没答应，只把口袋里的香肠纸揉成一团。第二天，屋顶上的折叠椅少了一把，Mitzi见了她也不再吹那两声口哨。

  Hermann Weiss 2来取货，把工钱一张张数在桌上。Rosa第一次没有经过师傅的手接过钱。晚上收工后，Rosa把工具箱擦了一遍，又把订单簿翻到新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (第一笔自己的订单); mood 0.05999999999999994; reputation 0.05; flag +first_independent_commission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  Rosa在告解席前跪了很久，最后只说了一句：我不知道还有没有人在听。Hermann Weiss 2没有立刻回答，隔着帘子把一枚旧十字架放在窗台上。Klara在教堂门口等Rosa，看见Rosa出来时没有划十字，也没有问。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +confessed_doubt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; knowledge “the priest left a worn cross on the window ledge”_

  信封没封牢，Rosa在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

  隔壁家的男人病了一个月，Rosa每天替他们提水。没有人提报酬。后来那家人搬走时，把一把钥匙留给Rosa，说以后路过可以进来坐。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +neighborhood_help_

- **1931.01** `r13-edu.nursing-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-hospital/certificate-half-year-late` tags: education, nursing, medicine, care, training, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa通过了笔试，操作考却没过。Katharina Berg让Rosa留下来补实习，工资减半。Rosa每天提前一小时到病房，给病人擦身、换床单，手上起了湿疹，最后还是拿到了证书，只是比别人晚半年。Katharina Berg在证书背面写了一行字：慢一点也算到。她把证书收好。
  _effects: cash -1 (病房里的第一针); condition; role_

- **1931.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Rosa看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。

  _effects: knowledge “Schnitzler 的剧作在 Burgtheater 上演时，观众会把剧情与维也纳的医生、官员和审查制度对照着议论。”; relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; cash -3 (gallery ticket and coat check); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Rosa替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Rosa自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.039999999999999994; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1932.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Rosa缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  手术做到一半，器械护士发现纱布少了一块。Rosa烧了四天，伤口鼓起来，第二次剖开才把纱布取出来；主刀说这是“个体反应”，账单上又添了一笔。主刀查房时没有进病房，只让护士带话。

- **1933.11** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 14 -> 11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_diaspora_gossip; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Rosa把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Luise Huber把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Friedrich Huber在楼梯间坐了很久；Rosa在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Rosa的名字；此后邻居提起Rosa，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

  _effects: cash 4 (第一笔自己的订单); mood 0.05999999999999994; reputation 0.05_

  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child_

  点心纸包搁在膝上，Friedrich Huber说熟人多，慢慢问，别急。Rosa回家把这些话学给母亲听，母亲只叹了口气。那包糖后来自己家里分着吃了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; cash -1 (再买一包糖上门（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Josef Huber。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Friedrich Huber把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Rosa躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:218_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Hermann Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 10; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.take-over-family-shop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::shop-crisis/shop-closed` tags: task, commerce, shop, family_firm, succession, debt, vienna, urban
  Rosa把最后一批货折价卖给街口的贩子，招牌摘下来靠在门后。Rosa Lechner坐在空柜台后面，说这铺子开了三十七年，第一次白天上门板。Rosa没有接话，只把钥匙放在Rosa Lechner手边；两人听见街上的失业队伍从门口走过去。
  _effects: cash 4 (把家里的招牌重新钉上); condition; flag +closed_family_shop; role; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the shop traded for thirty-seven years before closing”_

- **1939.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Rosa去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1939.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.repair-shop` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Rosa用围裙裹着钟走到钟表铺，铺子里的老头把钟翻过来看了看，说要换发条，连工带料一克朗二十赫勒。她算了算这星期的菜钱，把钟又裹回围裙里提回家，搁在柜上，没再提修的事。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Rosa胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  主顾在交货前改了主意：城里时兴更轻的样式，胡桃木太沉。Rosa把做好的柜门卸下来，木料退不掉，只能抵给料行。Gustav Weiss问还做不做，Rosa说先把欠账还清。
  _effects: cash -2 (materials that could not be returned（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +commission_lost; knowledge “胡桃木柜门已经做好，主顾却改要轻样式，木料退不回料行。”_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Friedrich Huber的孩子在人群里哭，Rosa把Friedrich Huber往后推了一步，自己站到第七个位置。Emil Weiss 2数人头时，Rosa说“名单上是我”；Emil Weiss 2看了Rosa一眼，让士兵把Friedrich Huber放走。当晚，Rosa被关进钟楼下的储藏室。
  _effects: condition; flag +took_neighbors_place_as_hostage; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  Rosa敲到第三家时，门后的人说这月已经随过两份份子。再往上走，走廊里只剩炒菜的油烟味；Rosa在楼梯口站了一会儿，把帽子里的钱整理好，自己又添了一克朗。回到Mitzi Lechner家时，只说大家都难。

  Otto Huber敲谷仓门时，靴子上全是泥，手里攥着半张撕掉的地图。Rosa让他进来，用干草盖住脚印；Emil Hofer在楼上咳嗽了一声，没下楼。天亮前，Elisabeth Moser的摩托车在院外熄了火，Rosa把Otto Huber推进地窖，自己坐在门槛上削土豆。
  _effects: flag +hid_deserter_from_military_police; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1941.07** `r2-faith.refugee-clothing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-away-for-papers` tags: charity, refugee, clothing, aid, queues, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Kral把Friedrich Huber的临时证翻过来，说签发日期过了三天，不能领。Friedrich Huber说火车站的人把日期写错了，Luise Kral说那就去找火车站。Rosa把自己的围巾解下来递过去，Friedrich Huber没有接，转身走进雪里。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +refugee_denied_clothing_for_expired_papers; relation rival with person-luise-kral; knowledge “an expired temporary pass blocks clothing distribution”_

- **1942.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-paid-the-fees` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Hermann Lechner在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Rosa把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。
  _effects: cash 8 (多年后的一封家信); flag +sibling_repaid_tutoring; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005835_

  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Rosa让Berta Moser记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Berta Moser只说了一句，明早再核一遍。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth
  Rosa难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Katharina Huber；Rosa一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Friedrich Huber把钟表送进当铺。Rosa落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中13克朗记入债务）)_

- **1943.01** `hist15-occ.work-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/permit-granted` tags: occupation, work, permit, institution, shortage, history, vienna, urban
  Luise Kral在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Rosa拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Rosa把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Hermann Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

  Elisabeth Moser说这个月不添人，让Rosa把名字写在门房的纸板上。纸板上已经有四十多个名字，最上面一行被雨泡花了。Rosa写得很小，挤在一个“王”字旁边，Elisabeth Moser看了一眼说，回去等吧，别天天来。
  _effects: cash -1 (工头兜里的粉笔头（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +work13_name_on_the_gate_board; knowledge “the gate board already holds more than forty names”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1943.10** `r1-hc.asylum-admission::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/certified-against-will` tags: health, mental, asylum, consent, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  第二个医生说Rosa有伤人倾向，需要两名家属作保。Friedrich Huber不肯签，表格被推回来，走廊里的护工已经站到门边。Emil Weiss 2把钢笔帽合上，说那就按观察期处理，先住两周。
  _effects: cash -15 (两张证明和一支钢笔（其中15克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “a second signature can turn admission into detention”; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus_

- **1943.11** `r2-leisure.hunting-party::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::hunting-party-crisis/donated-the-venison` tags: leisure, hunting, accident, class, guilt, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa打中了一只雄鹿，Emil Weiss 2当场放血，血装进带来的铁桶，说一滴都不能浪费。Gustav Berg让人把肉分成份，先送汤厨房，再送孤儿院。Rosa分到一块后腿，回家路上看见排队的人，又把肉塞给了队伍末尾的老太太。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; reputation 0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +donated_venison_to_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “the soup kitchen has been serving venison since the hunt”_

  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Anton Weiss夹着烟走到门口。Rosa报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Rosa没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 6 -> 7; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 52 -> 50; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa在Anna Huber面前签下放弃继承的声明，Alois Lechner从内袋里摸出二十克朗推过来，纸币上还带着体温。Mitzi把房契往自己那边挪了挪。Rosa数也没数就把钱收好，出门时听见公证人办公室的门在背后合上，铜把手凉了一下手心，这二十克朗把兄妹之间的话买断了一截。
  _effects: cash 20 (放弃继承换来一笔现钱); relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +renounced_inheritance_

  Rosa跑了两趟区公所和医院，问清住院的费用，把家里存着的八克朗取出来又借了四克朗，才把Rosa Lechner送进病房。回来时屋子空了一半，她站在灶前，第一次不知道该先做什么。Friedrich Huber说这样也好，至少夜里能睡整觉。
  _effects: cash -8 (支付母亲住院的押金与费用); health 0.019999999999999997 (夜间不再需要频繁起身，睡眠好转); mood -0.02000000000000013; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

  Rosa把通知念给Berta Hofer听，Berta Hofer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。

- **1945.01** `r14-fam.sunday-rota::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rota-signed` tags: task, family, divorce, co_parenting, custody, vienna, urban, postwar
  两人在厨房桌上把星期几、几点、谁去接孩子一条条写下来。Elisabeth Moser写到星期三时停住，说那天要上晚班；Rosa把星期三换到星期四，又在旁边画了一个圈。Alois Winter在门口听着，最后把那张纸拿过去，用彩笔在周末那格画了一只小船。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +co_parenting_rota; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006752:child_

- **1945.02** `r1-cul.zoo::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/saw-the-pacing-wolf` tags: culture, zoo, animals, family, captivity, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa在狼笼前看见一只狼来回走同一条线，爪印把泥地磨出一道沟。Emil Huber说它关了十二年；Mitzi问为什么不放，Emil Huber说放出去也活不了。回家后Rosa梦见那道沟，第二天去图书馆查了一下午狼的产地。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +saw_stereotypic_wolf; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; knowledge “the wolf has paced the same line for twelve years”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1945.02** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/admitted-with-papers` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  火车在清晨进站，Elisabeth Moser把Rosa的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Rosa把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。
  _effects: cash -2 (the passport fee and the first week's rent); state_add mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:311; state_add mb17-remittance-web state:world-vienna-1912:312; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “临时居留纸要求在四十八小时内到区公所登记住址。”; flag +mb17_newcomer_active; flag -mb17_remittance_web_active; flag +mb17_remittance_web_active_

- **1945.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Gustav Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 8 -> 11; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010472_

- **1945.07** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Allgemeines Krankenhaus病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1945.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1946.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/half-scholarship` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, postwar
  校长看了Hermann Huber的算术本，说可以免掉一半学费。Rosa在办公室里坐了很久，反复确认“不是借的”；出来时，Hermann Huber正在操场上追一只皮球，鞋底已经磨穿了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 60 -> 38; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 0 -> 32; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 32 -> 65; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 6 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 2 (the first instalment of the school grant); reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Rosa能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 22; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 65 -> 63; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 0; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `r13-work.reference-letter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-refused` tags: work, reference, hiring, favour, reputation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Alois Huber说Rosa走的时候没有打招呼，现在写信等于打自己的脸。Rosa站在门房外，把来意又说了一遍，Alois Huber低头去拨炉子，不再接话。回家的路上，Rosa把准备好的一小包烟叶捏碎了，撒在路边的雪里。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +work13_reference_letter_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Rosa把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Rosa没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 14; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 22 -> 23; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011145_

- **1946.04** `r3-war.trench-foot::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::trench-foot-ward/learned-to-walk-again` tags: war, army, trench_foot, medicine, evacuation, vienna, urban, postwar
  病房里一排脚都架在木架上，Gustav Lechner端着碘酒从床尾走过去。Rosa的脚趾保住了，但走路时像踩着一团棉花；Grete Huber在邻床教Rosa用脚跟先落地，说这样看不出瘸。Gustav Weiss来时，Rosa当着他的面走了三步。
  _effects: condition; flag +learned_to_walk_again; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  第一年还有信，第二年起信越写越短。边境关闭那天，Rosa在邮局门口站到关门。Friedrich Huber的最后一封信只有一行：孩子问你还记不记得我们的门牌。Rosa把信读了四遍，回信的地址已经作废。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +letters_stopped_after_border_closed; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “the last letter asked whether the subject remembers the door”_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

  有人把一封刚从故乡寄来的信读出声，读到“他在维也纳大概忘了家里”时，桌边的人都笑了。Rosa也跟着笑，手却停在汤勺上；他想起自己上个月没有寄出的那张汇款单。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 14 -> 11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Friedrich Huber把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Friedrich Huber在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1947.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/name-on-list` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Rosa替Elisabeth Bauer 2把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Anna Huber翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Elisabeth Bauer 2听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Rosa替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

- **1947.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Elisabeth Bauer 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Elisabeth Bauer 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Friedrich Huber凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Rosa把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Rosa去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Friedrich Huber劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1948.01** `mb17-language.school-counter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/register-without-the-mother-tongue` tags: migration, language, school, child, humiliation, belonging, vienna, urban
  登记表上有一栏“母语”，Grete Stein看了Rosa一眼，说这里只填德语。Rosa把家乡的村名写在纸上，又被要求擦掉；Hermann Huber攥着他的袖口，走廊里的孩子学着他们的发音，笑声一直跟到楼梯口。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 23 -> 25; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 8; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 14; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 47; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_school_humiliation; knowledge “学校把孩子的母语记成了需要纠正的缺陷。”_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Helene Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 17 -> 12; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.02000000000000013_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Rosa能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 25 -> 29; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 8 -> 13; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Rosa把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 52 -> 56; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Elisabeth Bauer 2忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Rosa陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Elisabeth Bauer 2站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1948.03** `r2-law.mortgage-foreclosure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/copied-the-record` tags: finance, mortgage, auction, housing, court, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa没有钱赎，也没有钱请Josef Berg 3，于是自己去法院抄拍卖记录。书记员说记录不能抄，只能看；Rosa看了整整一下午，把买主的名字记在手背上。Berta Winter经过时看了一眼，说记住也没用，价钱已经定了。
  _effects: flag +recorded_foreclosure_buyer; knowledge “the auction price was set before the hearing opened”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1948.07** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Alois Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 27 -> 30; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_remove journeyman state:world-vienna-1912:95_

- **1949.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/observed-quarantine` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Berg 2在门框上画了一道白线，说十天内不许出门，菜和煤会有人送到楼梯口。Rosa把线内的鞋一双双收进柜子，数了数米缸，又数了数窗台上的药包。第八天，送来的面包已经发硬。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.029999999999999916; condition; flag +observed_quarantine; knowledge “the quarantine lasts ten days and food is left at the stairh”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1949.04** `r1-civ.amnesty-corridor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/name-on-amnesty-list` tags: law, amnesty, prison, politics, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Lechner把特赦名单抄在一张薄纸上，从法院走廊的窗缝递进来，说Rosa的名字在第三页，但要本人到庭签收。Marie Huber在门口核对身份，Anna Huber把钥匙在腰上转了一圈。Rosa问签了是不是就没事了，Friedrich Lechner说“签了再说”。
  _effects: mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +name_on_amnesty_list; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the amnesty list requires a personal signature in court”_

  工头让Rosa把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Rosa没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 13 -> 16; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 29 -> 30; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012660_

- **1949.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Rosa的裙角。Elisabeth Bauer 2的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Rosa咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1949.10** `r13-rel.neighborly-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::neighborly-help-war/place-in-the-queue` tags: relationship, neighbor, help, obligation, illness, vienna, urban, postwar
  配给队从肉铺排到街角，Friedrich Huber把自己前面的位置让给Rosa，说家里还有半袋土豆。Rosa买完面包，回头看见Friedrich Huber重新排到队尾，围巾上落了一层雪。
  _effects: cash 1 (借来的煤与一只手); role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1950.01** `mb17-language.school-counter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/child-corrects-the-teacher` tags: migration, language, school, child, humiliation, belonging, vienna, urban
  老师问Mitzi Huber会不会拼自己的姓，Mitzi Huber用维也纳话答了一句，办公室里的人都笑了。Rosa没听懂，只看见Mitzi Huber抬起下巴；出门后，Mitzi Huber小声把刚才的话翻译了一遍，说老师把“sch”念错了。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 30 -> 33; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 16 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 40 -> 46; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 8 -> 12; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_child_translated_for_parent_

- **1950.01** `r14-spec.neighborhood-curse::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-iron-box` tags: neighborhood, curse, rumor, fear, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa顺着红绳找到Gustav Weiss家的后院，土里埋着一只铁盒，盒里是Rosa家丢了两年的门牌和一缕头发。Gustav Weiss站在门口，说红绳不是咒人，是提醒自己别忘了那笔旧账。Gustav Kral 2在墙外听见，转身就走。Rosa把铁盒盖上，手心里全是土。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +spec14_curse_revenge; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; knowledge “the neighbour kept the missing house plaque buried in the ya”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 6 -> 8; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 0; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 56 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-sent` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  Rosa替Elisabeth Huber 2写信，把最后知道的部队番号、收容站和一处旧住址都写上去。Elisabeth Huber 2不识字，却要Rosa把“家里都好”念三遍，怕写错。信投进邮筒时，她伸手在筒口摸了一下；Rosa把回信地址又描粗了一笔。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1950.09** `r3-mig.family-reunification::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sibling-signed-affidavit` tags: migration, family, sponsorship, papers, debt, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anna Huber把担保书翻到最后一页，指着连带责任那一行让Hermann Lechner读。Hermann Lechner读完，还是签了名，说“总不能让Rosa一个人在那边”。Anna Huber收下纸，给了一张入境许可的号码牌。Hermann Lechner出门时把号码牌塞进Rosa手里，手心全是汗。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +sibling_signed_affidavit; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child; knowledge “the sponsor accepts joint liability for housing and support”_

  Hermann Huber说再等一年，等家里的债还掉一点。Rosa没有劝，只把晚饭多添了一勺；夜里Rosa听见Hermann Huber在隔壁翻书，煤油灯一直亮到后半夜。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 7 -> 12; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 60 -> 64; cash -1 (another year of food and lamp oil（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Hermann Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 13 -> 8; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 72 -> 77; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `r14-soc.borrow-from-usurer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/eighteen-kronen-at-twenty-percent` tags: task, criminal_debt, debt, money, fear, vienna, urban, postwar
  Alois Winter在钟表铺后间放债，桌上摆着一把算盘和一杯冷茶。Rosa借十八克朗，周息两成，押的是下个月的工钱。Alois Winter让Rosa把名字写在借据上，又让在名字旁边按一个指印。“按重一点，”Alois Winter说，“省得以后不认”。借据被Rosa折成小方块，塞进内袋，走路时一直硌着胸口。
  _effects: cash 18 (钟表铺后间的算盘); flag +criminal_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “the usurer charges twenty percent a week”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1951.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Elisabeth Bauer 3，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Elisabeth Bauer 3点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Mitzi Lechner在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil Berg 2把表格推过来，说市立葬仪只要三克朗，不立碑，骨灰合葬。Rosa在“同意”栏按了手印，回家把死者的一把梳子放进抽屉最里面；Berta 2来送粥时问葬在哪，Rosa说不知道，登记簿上只有一个号码。
  _effects: cash -3 (棺材铺的尺寸量错了（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +municipal_funeral; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003713:child; knowledge “a municipal burial is recorded as a number, not a grave”_

  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1952.01** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Rosa把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Ferdinand Bauer递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.03 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004054:child_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 8 -> 10; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 53 -> 50; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.01** `p10-migration-tasks.pawn-belongings/pawn-watch-and-coat` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, pawn, sale
  他把怀表和两件冬衣递上铁栅栏后的柜台，当铺的人翻过表壳、抖了抖衣领，报出九克朗，写在一张蓝纸当票上。他留下那套木工工具，把当票折成小方块塞进身份证后面，数钱时手指有点抖。
  _effects: cash 9 (当掉怀表与两件冬衣换来的现钱); mood -0.050000000000000044; knowledge “当铺赎当期限六个月，过期死当，蓝纸当票要收好”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1952.03** `r3-mig.passport-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-passport-exchange/window-closed-at-noon` tags: migration, papers, queue, bureaucracy, work, vienna, urban, postwar
  天没亮Rosa就到市政厅门口排队，前面已经有八十多人。铁门十一点才开，Berta Moser出来挂了一块牌子：今日号已发完。队伍没有散，雨把号码纸泡烂了。Berta Moser说下周四再来，Rosa算了一下，那天正好轮到自己上工。
  _effects: cash -1 (雨里的护照队（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +missed_passport_window; knowledge “passport numbers are exhausted before noon”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

- **1952.09** `r2-law.loan-shark::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/reported-to-police` tags: finance, debt, usury, poverty, threat, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa去警察局报案，Marie Huber先问有没有借据。Rosa说有，Marie Huber又问利息写没写进借据。听到“五分”时，Marie Huber把笔放下，说这归民事，不归刑事；Maria Weiss 2在门外等着，手里提着Rosa的缝纫机。
  _effects: flag +reported_shark_lender; role; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; knowledge “police treat the shark loan as a civil matter”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1952.09** `r3-mig.immigrant-neighborhood::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-without-two-sponsors` tags: migration, community, organization, mutual_aid, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Hofer 3翻到担保人一栏，说必须有两位老会员签字。Grete Weiss愿意签一个，另一个名字写了又划掉。Ferdinand Hofer 3把表推回来，说“不是不认老乡，是怕有人借了钱就走”。Rosa把一克朗会费收回口袋，听见里屋有人在分汤。
  _effects: flag +refused_mutual_aid_membership; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004093:child; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159; knowledge “membership requires two sponsors”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 26 -> 34; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 62 -> 59; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.court` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  职员把信翻过来看背面，说私人纠纷不归这里管，去法院。律师的写字间在隔壁楼，开价三十克朗。Rosa数了数证件夹里的钱，差得远。兄弟在门口说，那就等传票。Rosa把信收好，下楼时鞋跟磕在石阶上。
  _effects: mood -0.14; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷”_

- **1953.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Rosa问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: health -0.03 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159; skill_

- **1953.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa看Anton Weiss 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Anton Weiss 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Rosa门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Rosa打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1953.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-cart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Rosa把铁盒抱在怀里，其余东西都交给Helene Huber和车夫。楼道里挡路的自行车被挪到墙边，锅沿碰上车挡板，咣的一声。新住处门前没有台阶，省得再抬。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005495:child_

- **1953.05** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  Rosa数出八赫勒交给Friedrich Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Friedrich Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Rosa盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1954.01** `r1-work.market-stall-allotment::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/bought-the-middle-place` tags: commerce, market, stall, permit, rivalry, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa把五克朗折成小方块，压在Berta Moser的登记簿下面。Berta Moser没有抬头，只把粉笔往中间挪了半尺。第二天Rosa的摊子到了棚中央，Hermann Huber的苹果墙被挤到排水沟边，有几只果子滚进了泥里。旁边卖菜的妇人把秤往远处挪了挪，Hermann Huber弯腰捡苹果时低声骂了一句；Berta Moser装作没听见，只把粉笔头碾碎在鞋底。
  _effects: cash -5 (棚下的第三个摊位); reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +bribed_market_master_

  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Rosa把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 2; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 50 -> 54; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.relative-introduction/relative-introduction.never-mentions` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, relative, introduction
  点心纸包搁在膝上，Anna Huber 2说熟人多，慢慢问，别急。Rosa回家把这些话学给母亲听，母亲只叹了口气。那包糖后来自己家里分着吃了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; cash -1 (再买一包糖上门); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1954.06** `r3-mig.deportation-appeal::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/appeal-dismissed-no-new-facts` tags: migration, deportation, law, appeal, papers, vienna, urban, postwar
  复核结果下来，申诉驳回，理由是“未提出新事实”。Berta Winter把新的离境日期念了一遍，Gustav Moser站在楼梯口，说愿意作证Rosa在这里住了九年。Anton Winter把证词夹进档案，说“九年不算新事实”。
  _effects: mood -0.14000000000000024; condition; flag +appeal_dismissed; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; knowledge “nine years of residence is not new evidence”_

- **1954.09** `r2-leisure.duel-affair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::duel-affair-postwar/refused-duel-under-new-law` tags: leisure, duel, honor, violence, class, vienna, urban, postwar
  战后共和国通过法令，决斗按谋杀论处。Mitzi Lechner还是把两支枪带来了，说规矩比法律老。Rosa看着枪盒，想起报纸上那个被判刑的军官。Katharina Weiss站在树底下，大衣扣得整整齐齐，说今天可以不打，但话得收回。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.04000000000000001; condition; flag +refused_duel_under_new_law; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000431:child; relation colleague with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “the new republic prosecutes duels as murder”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Helene Huber排在前面，回头替Rosa看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Rosa用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005495:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.half` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  二楼办事处里很冷，职员把信纸对着窗光照了照，说笔迹没错。四十二克朗用纸包好。兄弟在走廊里把纸包接过去，数了一遍，说以后各过各的。Rosa把父亲的信收进证件夹。

  Rosa把Luise Kral和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Rosa把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

  Rosa把钱包塞进内袋，走了两条街才敢回头看。三克朗够买一周的煤，彩票也许什么都不是；但那天晚上，Rosa把彩票压在盐罐底下，像压着一张还没有兑现的明天。

  _effects: state_remove mb17-remittance-web state:world-vienna-1912:312; flag -mb17_remittance_web_active_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Rosa在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Rosa一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 2 -> 3; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 37 -> 43; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Rosa又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health -0.060000000000000005 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1956.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Alois Huber把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Rosa去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中3.25克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1956.03** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Helene Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 8 -> 11; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.05** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Rosa，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Rosa说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Rosa自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 25 -> 37; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016928_

- **1956.07** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +rent_installment_plan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1956.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Rosa点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Gustav Moser量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.04 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

- **1956.08** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Alois Bauer 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1957.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Mitzi Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

- **1957.01** `af17-luck.quiet-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-nine-kronen` tags: luck, windfall, daily, quiet, money, social, coincidence, vienna
  Rosa把钱包塞进大衣内袋，用那九克朗买了两袋煤、一双不漏水的靴子和半磅咖啡。第二天，歌剧院门口贴出一张手写启事，说一位老妇人在找一只鳄鱼皮钱包，里面是她丈夫留下的最后一张站票。Rosa路过时把围巾往上拉了拉，靴子很暖，走路却没有声音。
  _effects: cash 9 (留下的钱包里的钱); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 43 -> 58; condition; flag +kept_found_wallet; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1957.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa对Luise Winter说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Luise Winter把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006309:child_

  二楼办事处里很冷，职员把信纸对着窗光照了照，说笔迹没错。四十二克朗用纸包好。兄弟在走廊里把纸包接过去，数了一遍，说以后各过各的。Rosa把父亲的信收进证件夹。

  Rosa说这一周先不收费，Anton问为什么，Rosa说“想让你看看新添的妇女版”。一周后，Anton续订了半年，还介绍了楼上的裁缝。Gustav Huber在楼梯口说“你比公司会做生意”，Rosa说“公司只算纸钱”。
  _effects: cash -2 (订户名单上的红叉（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +delivered_free_week_

- **1958.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Luise Kral低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1958.08** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Winter 2把Rosa寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Rosa追到码头时，Friedrich Winter 2已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Rosa在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.07999999999999996_

- **1959.01** `r2-law.will-reading::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/settled-quietly` tags: law, inheritance, will, family, probate, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa没有争，把遗嘱交给Franz Kral 2存档，自己只拿了父亲的一只烟斗。Mitzi后来把房子卖了，钱分了一半送来。Anton Bauer说这院子以后没梨吃了；Rosa把烟斗点上，说树还在别人家院子里。
  _effects: cash 8 (遗嘱第三页被水泡过); flag +settled_inheritance_without_dispute; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006435:child; knowledge “the house was sold and the proceeds split in half”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-181_

  烟从阁楼窗口往外翻，Rosa用湿毛巾捂住口鼻，摸到楼梯口。Gustav Lechner瘫在门边，鞋底已经烧软；Rosa把人拖到院子里，自己的手掌粘掉一层皮。消防队来后，把烧黑的楼梯封了，谁也不能再上去。邻居的鞋底在门槛上留下两块黑印。
  _effects: health -0.08 (burnt palms and smoke inhalation during the rescue); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; role; cash -2 (bandages, ointment and a new shirt（其中1克朗记入债务）); knowledge “阁楼起火时烟比火先到，湿毛巾只能顶一会儿；把人拖出来以后手掌会粘掉一层皮。”_

  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Rosa把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。

- **1915.06** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Rosa Lechner正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Emil往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。

- **1917.12** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home

  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Emil靠在墙边等Katharina Bauer，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Emil开始发烧，Katharina Bauer把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Emil的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Katharina Bauer的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Emil站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Katharina Bauer在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Emil掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Emil才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1920.12** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  二楼窗口有人喊了一声，Emil抬头应着，脚却没动。铁环卡在Lena的鞋边，Lena说要再玩一圈。Emil把铁钩递过去，说明天借你。Lena这才挪开脚，用鞋尖把铁环拨到Emil面前。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.02_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Emil后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Emil没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Emil用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

  Otto Winter家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Otto Winter端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Emil后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Emil没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Emil用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。

- **1923.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Emil的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Emil把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Emil Bauer翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Emil。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1923.06** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Klara擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Emil把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Klara看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

  _effects: mood -0.02_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Emil用牙咬断，重新来。Josef Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Emil把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1925.01** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Berg先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Emil每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:46; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Emil就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Emil还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Emil在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

  Ferdinand Weiss 2看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Katharina Bauer求了半天，Ferdinand Weiss 2答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Elisabeth Bauer干了三天就走了，Emil留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Ferdinand Weiss 2才让他碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

  _effects: cash -12 (拉丁文的旧课本); flag +family_sold_ring_for_school; relation friend with person-anna-bauer; education secondary_

  Emil白天帮家里干活，只能夜里复习。煤油灯熏得眼睛发酸，第二天考试时，Emil把会做的题做完，剩下的凭记忆填满。成绩不高，但够升级。Anton Lechner在成绩单上写：尚有潜力。
  _effects: skill; condition; reputation 0.009999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +passed_exam_barely; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1926.09** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Emil把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); mood -0.03_

  _effects: skill; skill; reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +prepared_with_borrowed_book_

  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

  _effects: mood -0.08; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”_

  Anna Bauer让全班按座位排队，Friedrich Weiss 2在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Emil时，Anna Bauer替他把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。

  “换清洗剂要加钱。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，只让人拆了半扇木板。Emil站在窗口试了试风，知道这已经是能争到的全部。此后每天上午还好，过了中午，太阳穴就开始一跳一跳地疼；Emil把手指在围裙上搓热，继续排字，工钱一分没少。
  _effects: health 0.019999999999999997 (窗户拆掉一半后气味稍减); condition; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; cash 1 (保住工位，照常领日薪); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  Emil把叠成方块的纸条塞给Hedwig，第二天在课桌里找回同一张纸，边角压平了，字没有被打开过。放学时Hedwig从另一边走了，Emil坐在电车上，一直坐到终点站。

- **1930.01** `p9-work-career.reference/p9-work-career.reference.strong` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, reference, certificate
  Emil把新东家的信放在柜台上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne读完，抽出公司的信笺，蘸墨水写下“勤勉可靠，品行端正，因故离柜，并无纠葛”。写完吹干，盖了章，还多写一句“有需要可来函”。Emil把信纸夹进厚纸板，第二天一早就往玛丽亚希尔夫的新账房去了。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; flag +has_strong_reference_

- **1930.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Emil把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Josef站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Emil手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; reputation 0.03_

- **1930.03** `p9-work-printshop.printed-secret/ask-for-name` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, secret, handbill
  Emil把笔推过去，要来人在单子上写下姓名和地址。Grete Winter 2收起纸，把帽子压低，从后门走了，那晚机器一次也没开。第二天Katharina Moser 2问起，Emil只说人没来。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Grete Bauer 2把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Emil打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1930.09** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.follow-monkey` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  走到街角，猴子突然蹿上窗台，把人家晾的一串洋葱碰掉了。乐师连声道歉，Emil帮着捡洋葱，两人笑了一阵。他说自己在二三区之间转，哪天转到她住的那条街，给她摇一支不收钱的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1931.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lending.coal-money` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Alois Lechner说煤店的账拖不过去，向Emil借五克朗。Emil从工钱袋里抽出钱，Alois Lechner在烟盒背面写了借条，说发薪日一定还；两人把烟盒压在厨房的盐罐下面。那天晚上，Emil家的炉子只烧了半筐煤。
  _effects: cash -5 (lent five kronen for the neighbour’s coal bill); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; role; flag +loan_outstanding; knowledge “借条写在烟盒背面，压在厨房的盐罐下面。”_

- **1931.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Grete Bauer 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Grete Bauer 2敲开Emil的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1931.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Emil手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.06 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1931.11** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-report` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  样张放在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的吸墨纸上，对方只说了一句知道了。Franz Weiss从办公室出来时脸是白的，走过字盘时肩膀撞了Emil一下。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; cash 1.5 (雇主给的赏钱); reputation -0.15_

- **1931.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Emil胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Emil在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Emil的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Emil咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Emil手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Friedrich Bauer等在门口，问值不值，Emil只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.08000000000000002; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1932.06** `major-education-university-registration/matriculated` tags: education, university, migration, major
  注册处窗口前排了三十多人。Emil把中学毕业证、洗礼证明、居住证明和一份家庭收入证明一张张递进去，Elisabeth Bauer 4用放大镜看了一遍，收下四十五克朗，在名册上写下编号。开学前，Emil在利奥波德城租下一间朝院子的房，床板、煤炉和一张书桌，月租十二克朗。
  _effects: education higher; cash -45 (matriculation fee); cash -12 (first month rent for the student room); role; flag +matriculated; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1932.11** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Emil在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。

- **1932.12** `major-work-dismissal/leaves-before-dismissal` tags: work, dismissal, unemployment, major
  Emil在更衣室听见自己的名字被念到“下周停工”那一栏。没等账房把信封封好，Emil先开口说家里有事，要自己走。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Emil写一张自愿离职的纸，说这样双方都体面；Klara在门口摇头。走出厂门时，Emil的工票还在口袋里，辞工证明却没有。
  _effects: unemployment (left before the dismissal took effect); cash -5 (lost final settlement); role; flag +left_before_dismissal; relation colleague with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/forgiven` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在院子的水龙头边等到Ferdinand Winter，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Ferdinand Winter拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Emil问要怎样才能收回，Ferdinand Winter说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。
  _effects: reputation 0.039999999999999994; flag +apology_accepted; role; relation friend with person-ferdinand-winter; knowledge “the insult has already reached three courtyards”_

- **1933.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Emil把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1933.06** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Emil听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1933.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Alois Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004694_

  Emil把写了三遍的信塞进琴凳，又在散场前取了回来。Marie Huber问是不是有话要说，Emil说天气要变了；两个人隔着Berta Bauer的毛线篮坐到钟响。回家后，Emil把信烧了，灰倒进花盆，第二天那盆天竺葵就蔫了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; cash -1 (the parlour fee and a pot of geraniums); mood -0.02999999999999997; flag +unsent_letter_

- **1934.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil看Alois Winter盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Alois Winter愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Emil门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Emil打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004819_

  Emil在城里的工厂找到了活，每天天不亮出门，每个月寄回三克朗。Josef学会用拐杖自己做饭，还在门口摆了个修鞋摊；Emil回家过年，看见他坐在摊子后面，手上全是鞋油，笑起来还是小时候的样子。Katharina Bauer说，你走以后他反倒站起来了。
  _effects: cash 2 (弟弟的拐杖); flag +sent_money_home_to_disabled_sibling; role; skill; relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

  Katharina Bauer把登记簿摊在门房的小桌上，让Emil写下姓名和住址。Emil写完后，Katharina Bauer在名字旁边画了一个小十字，说下周去给孤老送煤。本子上前面几行，墨迹已经褪成了褐色。
  _effects: cash -1 (慈善会登记簿上的空行); reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +joined_charity_visiting_round; role; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1934.02** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home
  Katharina Bauer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Emil晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Emil和Grete Bauer 2按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Emil喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood 0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  Anton Berg 2把汇款单和一张退条一起推过来，退条上盖着“地址不明”。Emil说上个月还寄到过，Anton Berg 2说“上个月是上个月”。五克朗扣了两克朗手续费，退回来的钱只够买三天的面包。
  _effects: cash -5 (退回来的汇款单); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +remittance_returned_address_unknown; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; knowledge “returned remittances lose the fee twice”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

  回信没有到Emil手里。Rosa Winter的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Hedwig隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Rosa Winter再见到Emil时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Emil站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Katharina Bauer把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Emil去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

  Emil把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Emil的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

  Emil在人群外站到腿发酸，只隔着跳舞的人看了Luise Stein几眼。散场时Anton裹着围巾凑过来，Emil低声托Anton去打听Luise Stein的门第和姓氏，自己先出了酒馆，在冷风里等回话；一整晚没敢上前，鞋底粘着酒馆地板的糖渍。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.07999999999999996; mood -0.06999999999999962; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

  Maria Lechner只给了Emil三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Emil把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Emil，但乐队指挥记住了Emil每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:186; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:187; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1937.04** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Gustav Lechner 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Gustav Lechner 2在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1937.11** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1937.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Luise Kral在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Emil排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `state_transition:university-student/dropout` tags: university-student, education, student, university, youth, state, state_tick, university-dropout
  Emil从「大学在读」转入另一段处境：two failed years ended the course
  _effects: state_transition university-student -> university-dropout; education secondary; flag +university_dropout_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Emil把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Emil把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 42 -> 46; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 12 -> 15; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 55 -> 53; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/absorbed-again` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  画室的门一关，时间就又变成{artist}的。Emil坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Emil发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 35 -> 41; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 50 -> 52; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 10 -> 8; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.toolbox` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  仓库里一股潮味。Emil蹲下把工具箱打开，凿子用油布包着，刨子柄上缠着哥哥自己的麻绳。老板说人搬走两个月了。Emil把工具卖给旧货商，三克朗，买票时数了两遍。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉哥哥留下的工具换路费); mood -0.16000000000000003; health -0.03999999999999998 (奔波找哥哥，路费和体力都耗尽)_

- **1938.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Emil听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Josef扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.06 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Hermann Moser在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Hermann Moser敲开Emil的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1938.12** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Emil走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  画室的门一关，时间就又变成{artist}的。Emil坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Emil发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。

- **1939.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::care-sick-parent-war/care-sick-parent.paid-in-bread` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, vienna
  Emil用面包和煤票请Alois Winter每天来两次，Alois Winter把药碾碎拌在粥里。Katharina Bauer退烧后第一句话是问面包够不够；Emil说够，转身把柜子里最后一块糖收进药盒。药盒盖上沾着面粉，她用袖子擦了擦。
  _effects: cash -2 (coal tickets set aside for the neighbour nurse); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; role; flag +nurse_paid_in_bread_

- **1939.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Rosa Lechner把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Emil打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (高烧硬熬伤身); relation friend with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1939.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把Klara和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Emil把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1939.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Friedrich Bauer的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Friedrich Bauer在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Emil下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Friedrich Bauer端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1939.11** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Lena排在前面，回头替Emil看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Emil用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006885_

  Emil把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Emil知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

  画店老板把Emil的作品推回来，说尺寸不合墙面，题材也不合买主的口味。Emil抱着画走回阁楼，路上经过一家亮着灯的咖啡馆，玻璃里映出一个抱着旧画框的人，像一个迟到的搬运工。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 53 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 15 -> 14; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.integrity 65 -> 62; health -0.020000000000000018 (冬天阁楼里没有足够的煤)_

  画室的门一关，时间就又变成{artist}的。Emil坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Emil发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。

  Emil陪Grete Stein去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Grete Stein用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Emil没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-grete-stein_

- **1940.05** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Katharina Bauer却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Emil把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; flag +still_looking_for_room_
