  Emil只买了一份，把剩下的钱塞进袜筒。Luise Kral脸色不好看，Anna Huber过来打圆场，说“量力而行”。出门后，Emil Weiss 2问Emil为什么不都买，Emil说“我得先活着等到兑付那天”。
  _effects: cash -3 (爱国债券的第六期); flag +bought_minimum_war_bonds; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “the neighbor doubts the bonds will be repaid”_

- **1940.12** `r1-fam.friendship-betrayal::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/betrayal.stolen-letter` tags: social, friendship, betrayal, work, reference, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil把工头写的推荐信借给Lena看，说下星期去面试。面试那天，Katharina Moser 2说已经有人来过了，信上的名字是Lena。Emil回到家，Lena正在院子里洗衣服，抬头笑了一下；Emil把盆里的水泼在墙根，什么也没说。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; role; relation rival with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation_end friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +reference_letter_stolen; knowledge “朋友拿着偷来的推荐信，写了自己的名字。”_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anna Stein的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1941.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Grete Stein第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Maria Weiss 2在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账); relation kin with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; reputation 0.020000000000000004; condition; flag +kept_the_tobacco_shop; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Emil学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。{artist}嫌Emil变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Emil收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 55 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 4 -> 8; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 53 -> 49; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Emil被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.050000000000000044 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”_

- **1941.03** `r2-faith.moral-reform-patrol::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/burned-the-list` tags: moral_reform, patrol, prostitution, hypocrisy, scandal, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil把名单带到后院，划了一根火柴。纸烧到一半，Klara从巷口转出来，看见火盆里的灰。Emil说名单被风吹走了，Klara盯着Emil看了很久，最后说：那下个月的巡夜，你来带队。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +burned_moral_reform_patrol_list; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the patrol list named men from the parish council”_

  停火前十分钟，Luise Kral还让打最后一轮。Emil把炮弹推进膛，手却停在拉火绳上；Elisabeth Bauer 2说“别打了”，Emil说“不打要上军事法庭”。炮响后，对面没有还击，只有一只钢盔从壕边滚下去。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +fired_last_round_before_armistice; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; knowledge “the last shell was fired minutes before the armistice”_

- **1913.06** `statistical_infection`

- **1916.11** `p9-childhood-household.supper-bowl/caught-adding-ladle` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Lena的勺子刚碰到锅底，Friedrich Hofer就转过身来。两个人都没说话。Friedrich Hofer把自己的碗往桌子中间推了推，又拿起面包继续撕。Lena盯着碗里剩下的汤，一口一口喝完，没敢再抬头。
  _effects: mood -0.04; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1917.07** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Lena烧得翻来覆去，Josefine Hofer把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Lena偏头躲，Josefine Hofer就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

  Lena在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1930.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Otto Weiss先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Lena点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1930.06** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Lena把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Lena的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Grete Weiss；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Otto Weiss把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:110_

- **1931.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/mocked-by-younger-students` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena被年轻同学笑说口音重，念课文时全班哄笑。Lena把书合上，第二天没来。Katharina Berg托人带话，Lena只说家里忙，其实每天那个时辰都坐在河边，用树枝在泥地上划自己的名字。河水涨起来的那天，Lena在泥地上写的名字被冲掉了，Lena又写了一遍。
  _effects: mood -0.09; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1931.06** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Lena眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Maria Kral隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1931.08** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Lena自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1931.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把通知念给Josef Winter听，Josef Winter摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Lena去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.029999999999999985_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Lena在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Lena的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.ask-promotion/p10-edu-work-tasks.ask-promotion.conflict` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, promotion
  他和Franz Weiss在门口争了几句，对方说本子上的字号不合规矩，他回了一句你根本没核过夜班的箱数。Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens把本子留下，位置给了别人，第二天他被调去夜班。本子他再没见着。
  _effects: mood -0.11999999999999997; health -0.03 (改上夜班，作息颠倒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1932.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena上楼敲了Grete Bauer 2的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Grete Bauer 2把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Lena能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1932.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena数出八赫勒交给Hermann Moser，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Hermann Moser说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Lena盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1932.12** `vienna-life-depression/short-time` tags: adulthood, depression, work
  Lena改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Lena开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work); condition_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004607_

  _effects: cash -1 (咖啡钱); mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  波尔卡重新响起时，Lena走到靠墙的长凳前向Anna Lechner弯腰。姑母手里的扇子停了停，没说话。入场费和小费一共一克朗，Lena把找零塞回内袋；一支舞跳完，Anna Lechner说了自己住在哪条街，Lena把名字和门牌在心里念了两遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (付给乐队的小费与舞会入场费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  窗口在十二点关上。Gustav Berg把材料推回来：拉丁文课时不够，中学毕业证上的印章也模糊。Lena问能不能先注册再补，Gustav Berg说下一位。走出注册处时，雨把税单打湿了一角；四十五克朗还在口袋里，但名册上没有编号。
  _effects: cash -6 (certified copies and stamps); reputation -0.04; flag +matriculation_rejected; role; mood -0.08000000000000002_

  Hermann Moser把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Lena留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Lena在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +union_signature_blacklisted; knowledge “the foreman punishes union signatures with the worst furnace”_

  Lena把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Lena走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -6 (门牌号与登记簿); condition; flag +paid_registration_and_doorplate_fee; relation colleague with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the blue doorplate signals a registered address”_

  Lena对Gustav Weiss说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Gustav Weiss把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Lena的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000306:child_

  工头把 Lena 叫到车床后面，声音压得低，手上的铅笔在工牌背面点了两下。Lena 解释时，Mitzi 正把一箱零件搬远，肩膀缩着，始终没往这边转头。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.work-injury/rag-and-work` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  棉纱缠了三圈，橙色碎屑沾在布面上。Lena用左手推料，右手悬着不敢使劲，每压一下冲床，指根就跳一下。工头走过来瞥了一眼，说这才像干活的样子。
  _effects: health -0.06 (手指伤口未妥善处理); condition; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Emil点了牛奶咖啡，Lena要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Lena的裙角。Grete Stein的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Lena咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Lena把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; flag +union_ledger_public_

  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +novel_shelved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Lena躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Lena去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1945.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Anton Lechner。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Hermann Lechner把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:310_

- **1945.01** `af17-luck.quiet-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-wallet-was-a-plant` tags: luck, windfall, daily, quiet, money, social, coincidence, vienna
  钱包刚放进内袋，一个戴圆顶礼帽的男人就抓住Lena的手腕，说Lena偷了他的钱。围观的人越多，男人说得越响；直到街角的巡警认出他上个月在同一位置演过同一出戏。Lena拿回自己的围巾，丢了一克朗的车钱，也学会了先看衣领再看钱包。
  _effects: cash -1 (被缠住后错过的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 5 -> 10; reputation -0.020000000000000004; skill; condition; flag +wallet_was_bait_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Lena，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Lena说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Lena自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 10 -> 22; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010353_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Lena在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Lena一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 2 -> 3; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 22 -> 28; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Lena谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 30; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1945.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.sister` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  姐姐把孩子裹在披肩里抱上电车，说城外有院子，一个月就送回来。Lena站在站台上看车走远。一个月后孩子会叫人了，可看见Lena就往姐姐身后躲。Lena把孩子抱进屋，他哭了半宿。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Grete Winter 2起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1946.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Grete Lechner。接生的人收六克朗，Hermann Lechner在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:325_

- **1946.01** `cj17-police.lineup::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-man` tags: police, court, legal, investigation, mistaken_identity, humiliation, vienna, urban
  预审室里点着三盏煤气灯，Klara从五个男人面前走过，在第四个人面前停住，又退回第二个。Anna Huber把Lena的帽子摘下来看了看，最后在记录上写了“排除”。Lena走出警局时，天还没亮，扫街的人正在冲洗台阶。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 30 -> 20; reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +cleared_in_lineup_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Lena错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 3 -> 2; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 28 -> 38; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Lena谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 20 -> 35_

- **1946.01** `r13-fam.ask-neighbour-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/work-in-exchange` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara说煤可以借，但要Lena明天清早帮着把地窖里的土豆搬上来。Lena答应了，蹲在门槛上把数目写在自己的手心里。Klara看见那几个歪字，笑了一下，多给了半筐碎煤。
  _effects: food; flag +worked_for_borrowed_coal; role; skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1946.03** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Lena听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1947.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Hermann Lechner在楼梯间坐了很久；Lena在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Lena的名字；此后邻居提起Lena，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

- **1947.01** `fm17-saint.first-cure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-official-offers-a-license` tags: faith, healing, medicine, saint, investigation, child, reputation, money
  Anna Huber说，如果Lena不再称这是奇迹，可以在市立诊所登记做一名护理助手：有工钱，有制服，也有上级。Lena看着那张表格，想起调查员问过的话，最后在“护理助手”下面签了名，却把“不得施行祝福”那一行折进了纸里。
  _effects: state_add fm17-saint-or-fraud state:world-vienna-1912:342; role; cash 1 (护理助手的第一笔工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; flag +fm17_healing_tolerated; flag +fm17_public_healer_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Lena和Josef Berg按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Lena喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1947.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Friedrich Winter在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Friedrich Winter敲开Lena的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1947.12** `r15-love.borrowed-room::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refuses` tags: love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Hofer在第三次见面时说不能再这样。Lena没有挽留，只把对方落下的手套收进抽屉；后来每次打开抽屉，都能看见那双手套，却想不起最后一次见面时对方穿的是什么外套。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  一个孩子在被“祝福”后的第二天烧得更厉害。家里人没有闹，只把那条擦过额头的布巾留在门口；Lena把它捡起来，洗了，又晾回去，像是在等一个不会来的解释。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-saint-or-fraud.following 10 -> 5; state_parameter fm17-saint-or-fraud.suspicion 10 -> 20; cash -1 (请医生来家里看孩子); reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `r13-civ.ration-card::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ration-card-issued` tags: politics, rationing, food, war, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Moser把配给证从本子上撕下来，填上Lena家的门牌和人数，又用打孔机在角上打了一个洞。Lena把证举到灯下，看见上面印着面粉、糖和煤的数量，每一项都比去年少。
  _effects: food; flag +ration_card_issued; knowledge “the monthly ration is smaller than last year’s”_

- **1948.10** `r3-war.armistice-eleven::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::first-armistice-day/told-armistice-story` tags: war, armistice, ceasefire, aftermath, memory, vienna, urban, postwar
  纪念仪式上，Helene Weiss问Lena当年停战时在做什么。Lena说在擦枪，擦到一半听见外面安静了，还以为是耳朵坏了。Helene Weiss笑，Lena也笑，笑完把手里那支没点的烟放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +told_armistice_story_to_neighbor; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child_

- **1949.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Elisabeth Lechner；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Hermann Lechner把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:374_

- **1949.01** `state_expire:fm17-saint-or-fraud` tags: fm17-saint-or-fraud, faith, healing, saint, fraud, reputation, religion, medicine
  Lena的「被当成圣人或骗子的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fm17-saint-or-fraud state:world-vienna-1912:342_

- **1949.01** `r13-work.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/joint-fits` tags: work, craft, practice, wood, skill, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把第四十个榫头敲进卯眼，声音是闷的，不晃。Franz Bauer 2路过，用拇指按了按接缝，说这回不用胶也能站住。Lena把废掉的三十九个榫头扫进灶膛，火苗从锯末里窜起来，屋里全是松木的味。
  _effects: cash 2 (第四十个榫头); flag +work13_forty_practice_mortises; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1949.03** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Lena端着一只旧锅去敲Katharina Winter 2的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Katharina Winter 2让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Lena连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1949.05** `r3-crime.petty-theft-arrest::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/citation-at-the-door` tags: crime, police, poverty, law, shop, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Berg 2把Lena的袖子捏在手里，先数篮子里的面包，再数口袋里的硬币。Berta Berg站在门口说“少了两只”，Rosa Bauer 2却记得货架上本来就空着。Emil Berg 2把一张蓝色传票塞进Lena手里，说下周三上午九点，别迟到。
  _effects: cash -3 (后巷里数面包的手（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +citation_for_petty_theft; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; knowledge “the baker counts the missing bread as theft”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Hermann Lechner在楼梯间坐了很久；Lena在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Lena的名字；此后邻居提起Lena，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1950.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  厂门口的砖墙被太阳晒得发烫。Alois Bauer在旁听着，插了一句这后生看着手稳。工头把烟灰弹进阴沟，说试半天，午饭他管。Lena点头时，肚子先响了一声。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000306:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1950.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Lena说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Karl Kraus一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-karl-kraus_

- **1950.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Berta Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Lena把床单对折，腾出一半，Berta Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.12** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Lena在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Hermann Lechner在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/enrolled` tags: childhood, school, fees

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  掌柜不肯赊，Rosa Lechner 2从队尾挤过来，说这户人家的男人在印刷所做了十年，欠不了一筐煤。掌柜把“两赫勒”写在门牌号旁边，煤只装了小半筐。Anton抱着筐经过Rosa Lechner 2身边，Rosa Lechner 2替Anton把绳结紧了紧。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1918.10** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1918.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/punished` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  Anton在课堂上和同桌说话，Elisabeth Stein用戒尺打了手心。孩子把红肿的手藏在桌下，放学后不敢让家里人看见；此后听见戒尺敲桌沿，Anton的肩膀就先缩一下，对Elisabeth Stein也多了几分怕。
  _effects: condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Elisabeth Stein家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Anton递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Elisabeth Stein端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Anton把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; skill_

- **1919.04** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Anton被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Anton一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Rosa提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

  Anton在放学路上把教师儿子推倒在雪里，两个人滚成一团。Elisabeth Stein赶来时，先扶起自己的儿子，再问Anton为什么打人。Anton说他先切我的橡皮。Elisabeth Stein说一块橡皮也值得动手？那天Anton在走廊站到天黑。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +fought_teachers_son; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一捆教区通告交给Josefine Hofer带回家。Anton放学后在厨房桌上折页到夜里，手指沾满油墨，两周后交回六千张，拿到三克朗二十赫勒；钱交给Josefine Hofer时，Anton的指尖已经洗不干净，指甲缝里留着一圈黑；家里从此多了一个能挣几个铜板的孩子。

  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; food_

  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Anton靠在墙边等Josefine Hofer，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Anton开始发烧，Josefine Hofer把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Anton的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Josefine Hofer的手指也裂了口子。

  Anton把书藏在课桌里，被Ferdinand Weiss 2没收。Ferdinand Weiss 2翻了两页，发现是讲蒸汽机的，放学后把书还回来，说下次别在课上读。Anton问能不能借老师的书，Ferdinand Weiss 2说先把这本读完。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Anton的肚子在办公室里叫了一声。Anton Lechner问早饭吃了什么，Anton说喝了水。Anton Lechner从抽屉里拿出半块面包，让Anton吃完再讲题。题讲完，面包还剩一口，Anton用纸包好，带回去给妹妹。妹妹问面包是哪来的，Anton说是老师给的，妹妹不信，还是吃完了。

  Anton交了钱，Anna Bauer却把小学毕业证推回来，说印刷模糊，要他下星期三补一份学校盖章的副本。报名表压在桌角，夜班名额只留到星期五。他连跑两趟学校，第二趟才找到管事的人。
  _effects: cash -5 (报名手续费与押金); mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +needs_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1928.11** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-sign-up-hike` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Anton把十赫勒放在桌上的铁皮盒里，翻到歌本最后一页，用铅笔写下自己的名字。Hedwig在后面戳Anton的背，说乡下的草地能躺着听云。
  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费); relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anton交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1930.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Emil Winter 2点了牛奶咖啡，Anton要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1930.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Anton自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Anton废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1930.01** `major-task.marriage-proposal/proposal-refused` tags: task, task:family, family, marriage, courtship, household
  Berta Weiss把戒指推回来，说自己的工钱还要寄给乡下的母亲，不能现在就成家。两人在长椅上坐到天黑，最后一起走到电车站；第二天在楼道里碰见，彼此都客气了许多。Anton把戒指重新包进手帕，放回抽屉。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; flag +marriage_proposal_refused; cash -2 (tram fare and coffee at the riverside); knowledge “对方的工钱要寄回乡下，短期内不能成家。”_

- **1931.01** `r15-work.journeyman-test::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-with-praise` tags: work, craft, apprentice, exam, journeyman, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton的成品被留在作坊临街的窗台上，路过的人都能看见。Friedrich Huber说这不是最好的活，但是最像Anton自己的活。
  _effects: mood 0.08; reputation 0.1; flag +promising_journeyman; state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> journeyman; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; role_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002194_

- **1931.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/switched-to-numbers` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Anna Bauer说识字班已经满员，问Anton要不要改上算术课。Anton点头。课上讲的是工钱、尺码和利息，Anton把每一道题都抄在烟盒背面。月底算工钱时，Anton第一次发现工头少记了两个钟头，拿着纸去问，工头把硬币补了回来。算术课学费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_numeracy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002188_

- **1931.01** `r13-edu.foreign-language::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/first-conversation` tags: education, language, foreign_language, study, culture, medical, university, knowledge
  Anton每天清晨跟着Katharina Berg念法语，把动词变位写在手背上。三个月后，Anton在咖啡馆替一位法国客人指路，对方说的“谢谢”Anton听懂了。回家路上，Anton把这句话重复了一路，连买菜时都在心里默念。第二天Anton提前半小时到了课堂。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +foreign_language_certificate; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1931.06** `kernel-health.addiction::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/addiction-withdrawal` tags: health, addiction, morphine, hospital, work, reform, medical, schooling
  戒断的第三天，Anton把床单咬出了洞，浑身像被冷水浇过。Marie Huber按着肩膀，不让去翻药柜；熬到天亮，人才睡过去。醒来第一句话是问今天几号。他喝了一碗汤，手还抖得端不稳，汤洒在床单的洞上。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -4 (food, clean linen and lost work during withdrawal); relation colleague with person-marie-huber; knowledge “突然停用吗啡会发冷、呕吐、整夜睡不着；有人按着肩膀才能熬过去。”_

- **1931.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Anton看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.05; health -0.04000000000000001 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1931.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.03_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004080_

- **1932.01** `r15-work.first-commission::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/paid-in-full` tags: work, craft, journeyman, commerce, reputation, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Huber来取货，把工钱一张张数在桌上。Anton第一次没有经过师傅的手接过钱。晚上收工后，Anton把工具箱擦了一遍，又把订单簿翻到新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (第一笔自己的订单); mood 0.06; reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +first_independent_commission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1932.01** `r13-fam.ask-neighbour-help::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/work-in-exchange` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Otto Weiss说煤可以借，但要Anton明天清早帮着把地窖里的土豆搬上来。Anton答应了，蹲在门槛上把数目写在自己的手心里。Otto Weiss看见那几个歪字，笑了一下，多给了半筐碎煤。
  _effects: food; flag +worked_for_borrowed_coal; role; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1932.02** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  教堂后院的铁锅冒着白汽，排队的人把各种罐子捧在胸前。Luise Kral穿着灰围裙一勺一勺地分，轮到Anton时锅底已经露出来，勺子刮出响声，还是刮了半勺倒进罐里。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1932.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.03 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1932.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Anton下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Anna Bauer从字母表教起，Anton把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Anton第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Anton用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002745_

- **1933.01** `r15-work.first-commission::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/kept-for-family` tags: work, craft, journeyman, commerce, reputation, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton没有把成品卖出去，而是搬回了家。家里有人说这能换钱，Anton说先放着。那张桌子后来用了很多年。
  _effects: mood 0.05; flag +kept_first_masterwork_

- **1933.01** `r13-edu.nursing-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/finds-vein-first-try` tags: education, nursing, medicine, care, training, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton第一次给病人扎针，手抖得厉害，Grete Stein让Anton先在自己胳膊上试。Anton扎了两次才回血，病人反倒安慰Anton。三个月后，Anton能闭着眼找到血管，白大褂口袋里总装着一小卷胶布和一块剪好的纱布。Grete Stein在排班表上把Anton的名字往前挪了一格。
  _effects: cash 2 (病房里的第一针); flag +nursing_certificate; role; skill_

- **1933.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.crooked-sleeves` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Anton的剪刀钝得咬布，一剪下去袖口走了斜线。她索性把两只袖子都裁短，袖边用灰线锁了一圈。锁边时线太短，接到第三段才够，右手食指被针顶出个红印，改完像件旧式的短褂。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1933.12** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Anton把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Anton拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1933.12** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Franz Winter 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Franz Winter 2在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; mood 0.04000000000000001; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Anton在图书馆借了一本被列入禁书目录的小册子，登记时用了假名。第二天，Gustav Berg把Anton叫到柜台后面，翻开借阅卡，指着那个名字。Anton说只是好奇，Gustav Berg把书收回柜里，说下不为例，声音压得很低。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; flag +library_restricted_book; relation rival with person-gustav-berg_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003915_

- **1934.01** `wc17-commission.masterwork::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/design-copied` tags: craft, commission, masterwork, workshop, reputation, patronage, vienna, urban
  图样在交货前被Lena带去另一家作坊。Anton在市场上看见同样的书柜，铜边更薄，价格低三成；买主不知道差别，只说这家做得快。Anton把尾款收下，回到作坊把原图锁进柜子。
  _effects: state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:144; cash 2 (reduced commission payment); reputation -0.03; mood -0.05000000000000002; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +masterwork_copied; knowledge “市场上那把书柜用了我的铜边图样，但榫头更薄。”; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

- **1934.01** `r13-edu.borrow-instruments::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/instrument-borrowed` tags: education, instruments, loan, science, trust, medical, university, knowledge
  Grete Stein打开仪器柜，让Anton自己挑。Anton借了一台显微镜，用绒布包好，抱在怀里走回家。路上有人撞了Anton一下，Anton第一反应是弯腰护住镜子，书掉了也没管，到家才发现膝盖磕破了皮。那本书是借来的，却比镜子先被忘在门口。
  _effects: flag +instrument_borrowed; skill_

- **1934.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

  到期日过了三天，Rosa Berg带着法院的人上楼，把缝纫机和两只木箱抬到走廊里登记。Friedrich Hofer把一张粉色的纸贴在镜子上，说七天内可以赎。Anna Lechner把门开了一条缝，又轻轻合上。Anton蹲在地上捡散落的线轴，线缠住了手指。

  Anton在图书馆借了一本被列入禁书目录的小册子，登记时用了假名。第二天，Franz Weiss把Anton叫到柜台后面，翻开借阅卡，指着那个名字。Anton说只是好奇，Franz Weiss把书收回柜里，说下不为例，声音压得很低。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  _effects: cash -2 (materials that could not be returned); mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +commission_lost; knowledge “胡桃木柜门已经做好，主顾却改要轻样式，木料退不回料行。”_

  Franz Winter 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Anton留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Anton在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06; role_

  他在南站买了一张三等车票，十二克朗四十赫勒，把木工工具留给了同屋的工友。车过布吕恩时换了一次车，哥哥站在站台上，手里拎着一壶水。第二天一早他就跟着下地，手上磨出了新泡。

- **1942.04** `r3-war.refugee-train::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bribed-guard-for-seat` tags: war, refugee, train, station, migration, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton用三克朗从Emil Weiss 2手里买了一个靠窗的位置，把Josef Berg推上去。Emil Weiss 2把钱塞进袖口，说“别让人看见”；Anna Huber过来查票时，Anton把自己的票举得高高的。
  _effects: cash -3 (站台上的第十节车厢（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +bribed_guard_for_refugee_seat; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1942.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton把通知念给Emil Hofer 2听，Emil Hofer 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Anton去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

  Emil Berg 2在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Anton拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Anton把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

- **1943.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Katharina Moser在午休时把本子摊在Anton的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Anton从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Katharina Moser当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002118:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  Anton的作坊又接了两单：一扇橡木门、一只修到一半的八音盒。Anton把工钱先付给学徒，自己留下最后一张钞票，用铅笔在账本背面记下欠料行的数目。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 3 -> 5; cash 2 (workshop commissions); reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/no-record` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Emil Weiss 2把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Luise Kral不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Anton扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); mood -0.17999999999999972; condition; flag +missing_relative_no_record; relation kin with person-luise-kral; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”_

- **1943.05** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  警报响时Anton躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

  Friedrich Bauer没有商量，把存折拿去给Anton的兄弟做担保。Anton发现时，存折上多了一行陌生人的名字和一枚蓝章。Friedrich Bauer说只是借三个月；Marie Huber在柜台后听见，低头把算盘珠子拨回去，没有抬头。
  _effects: cash 10 (存折上的蓝格子); condition; flag +passbook_used_as_collateral; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; knowledge “the passbook now guarantees a stranger’s loan”_

  主顾在契约上按了印，要求一把带黄铜镶边的胡桃木书柜。Anton带着Rosa Moser做了四十天，刨花堆到窗台；交货那天，主顾把尾款数了两遍，又在工匠名册上添了Anton的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 师傅作坊 (household_firm); position 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009801; state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:299; cash 4 (masterwork commission fee); skill; reputation 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; knowledge “主顾把尾款数了两遍，并把我的名字写进了工匠名册。”; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

  Anton走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.03** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  玛丽亚在市场拱门下拦住Anton，声音不大但周围人都听得见：三个克朗。Anton翻遍口袋只摸出几个十字币。玛丽亚把布袋从Anton胳膊上解下来，转身走了。
  _effects: food; reputation -0.15000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Anton在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Elisabeth Moser把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Anton躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Anton去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

  主顾在交货前改了主意：城里时兴更轻的样式，胡桃木太沉。Anton把做好的柜门卸下来，木料退不掉，只能抵给料行。Katharina Moser问还做不做，Anton说先把欠账还清。
  _effects: cash -2 (materials that could not be returned（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; knowledge “胡桃木柜门已经做好，主顾却改要轻样式，木料退不回料行。”_

  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Anton守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 2 -> 5; cash -1 (workshop winter costs)_

  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +donated_coat_returned; relation friend with person-rosa-lechner_

  _effects: position_end 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006864; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; flag +kept_office_keys; knowledge “储藏室有两箱以我的名义订阅的期刊，收件地址仍是这间办公室。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  他的申请被退回，理由是“担保人收入不足”。Anna Huber说可以找第二担保人；他翻遍通讯录，发现能签字的人不是已经走了，就是不肯再担保。回家后，他把通讯录上能签字的名字一个个划掉。
  _effects: flag +emigration_papers_refused; cash -2 (照片、表格和往返车钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; reputation -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “护照申请需要担保人收入证明，找不到第二担保人就办不下去”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

  Hermann Moser抱着孩子连声道谢时，Anton已经弯着腰在咳。药房玻璃瓶上贴着褪色标签，喝下去满嘴甘草味；后半夜Anton听见自己牙齿打颤，炉子早灭了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Anton把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Gustav Kral 2替Anton扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

  图样在交货前被Gustav Moser带去另一家作坊。Anton在市场上看见同样的书柜，铜边更薄，价格低三成；买主不知道差别，只说这家做得快。Anton把尾款收下，回到作坊把原图锁进柜子。
  _effects: state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:341; cash 2 (reduced commission payment); reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; knowledge “市场上那把书柜用了我的铜边图样，但榫头更薄。”; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

  图书馆的目录柜卡住，Anton Moser用一把小刀撬开抽屉。他要找的那本地方志只剩半册，借书卡上最后一个名字是三十年前；他坐在窗边抄到闭馆，手指被纸边割了一道。管理员说这半册不能复印，只能一页一页抄。
  _effects: knowledge “地方志只剩半册，借书卡上最后一个名字是三十年前，闭馆前可以抄完”; reputation 0.03999999999999998; role; cash -1 (抄纸和存包费); flag +found_archive_document; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Anton没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002385:child_

  旅馆老板Mitzi把一张订单摊在刨床上：四十张床架，月底交货。Anton算了一遍工时，按现在的做法只能保本；第二天，Anton把Emil Moser的午饭时间缩短，又把工钱改成按件计。Emil Moser没有反对，只在木料上多画了一道线。

  毯子吸饱了水，沉得像一条死狗。Luise Moser 2说这毯子是她婆婆的嫁妆，一年只洗一回。Anton咬着牙拧到最后一圈，水从毯缝里挤出来，在脚边汇成一小片温热。

  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

  Grete Huber从芝加哥寄来一封信，里面有一张屠宰场的招工单和一个地址。信纸背面写着“来的时候带一双厚鞋”。家里卖了一头牛，凑出船票钱。Anton把招工单缝进衣领，Otto Weiss 2说这种信不能给海关看。
  _effects: cash 6 (先走的那个人寄回了地址); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +chain_migration_letter; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the cousin has a job order at a Chicago slaughterhouse”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

  投票结果是十九比十七。Anton把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Anna Huber在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Anton的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009801; reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.07999999999999996; condition; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 55 -> 60; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 35 -> 33; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 10 -> 12; cash 2 (workshop profit)_

  药房说没有货，Anton把处方叠好，去了下一家。天黑时手里只有一包阿司匹林和一瓶咳嗽糖浆；Emil Bauer说总比空手强，把糖浆让给了孩子。回家后他把阿司匹林分成两半，糖浆瓶口用纸塞着，漏了一点。
  _effects: cash -8 (aspirin and cough syrup after a day of searching（其中5克朗记入债务）); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1949.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Anton把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1949.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Hermann Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Hermann Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002651:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `wc17-sweatshop.ledger-and-loyalty::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/child-on-the-floor` tags: sweatshop, ownership, family, inspection, work, factory, vienna, urban
  Anton Bauer放学后留在车间里数扣子，一只手指被针扎穿。Anton抱起Anton Bauer往诊所跑，路上想起自己十二岁那年也在同一排机器前站过；第二天，Anton把Anton Bauer送回了学校。
  _effects: cash -2 (clinic fees for the child（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 12 -> 27; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 33 -> 28; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 60 -> 58; mood -0.030000000000000138; flag +child_injured; knowledge “孩子的手指被针扎穿，我十二岁时也站在同一排机器前。”_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Anton让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 27 -> 37; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 58 -> 56; cash -2 (worker compensation（其中2克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013120_

- **1950.01** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/quarrel-with-clerk` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Anton和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Anton在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: reputation -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/inspection-week` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Anton把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 45 -> 60; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 56 -> 53; cash -1 (inspection fine（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/broke-quarantine` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton等到夜里，从后院的柴门溜出去上工。车间里没有人问，机器照常转，只有换班的人往旁边让了半步。回家时白线还在，Anton跨过去，鞋底带回一层湿泥。
  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.1; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”_

- **1950.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton没有立刻答话，先去敲Anna Huber 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anna Huber 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Anton把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

- **1950.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Emil Bauer合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1951.01** `wc17-sweatshop.ledger-and-loyalty::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/guards-and-shorter-hours` tags: sweatshop, ownership, family, inspection, work, factory, vienna, urban
  检查员走后，Anton让机修工给每台机器装了护板，又把学徒的工时从十二小时减到十小时。账本上这个月的利润少了一截；但夜班结束时，没有人再把手伸到皮带下面。
  _effects: cash -3 (guards and shorter hours（其中3克朗记入债务）); state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 60 -> 45; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 28 -> 38; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 37 -> 29; reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +improved_workshop; knowledge “装了护板、减了工时以后，夜班没有再出过手被卷住的事。”_

- **1951.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Maria Weiss 2，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Maria Weiss 2点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Mitzi Lechner在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Anton把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

  _effects: cash 1 (铁门开了一条缝); flag +work13_hired_after_trial_at_gate; role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; employment Bohrer @ org-winter-print_

- **1952.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.05** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

  前面还有七八个人时，Marie Huber已经直起腰把锅盖扣上了。队伍没散，有人骂了一句，有人蹲下把罐子收进布袋。Anton走到巷口，Otto Moser 2从后面赶上来，罐子还温着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Anton不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Anton拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Anton的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.11999999999999998 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

  Anton输了五票，要求把票重新数一遍。数到第二遍还是五票，Anna Huber把铁盒盖上，说“再数就难看了”。Anton说账本也得查，后厅一下子安静下来。那晚的合唱没有唱成。散会后，Anna Huber把铁盒抱在怀里，锁进酒馆老板的柜子。铁盒被放回柜子，账本却留在了桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.05; flag +disputed_club_election; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1953.02** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子)_

  Anna Huber先去了委员会，说Anton经手的账目对不上。委员会让Anton把收条全部交出来，Anton在自己抽屉里发现少了一叠。走廊里有人低声说“看着挺老实的”。调查没有结论，但募捐箱换了锁。换锁那天，Anton把旧钥匙交出去，钥匙在桌上转了两圈才停。那把钥匙被丢进抽屉，再没有配过新的。

  Alois Bauer 3嫌Anton的床单滴水滴到Alois Bauer 3家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Anton下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Anton把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Alois Bauer 3那侧。

  合唱团在酒馆后厅改选，票箱是一只装饼干的铁盒。唱票唱到第三遍，Anton比Otto Stein多三票。Otto Stein把账本推过来，说“你接”。散会后，两人在门口抽完同一支烟，谁也没有先提那三票。账本第一页记着上次义卖的收入，数字旁边有一道被擦过的铅笔痕。
  _effects: mood 0.12; reputation 0.08; flag +won_club_election; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1959.02** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton上楼敲了Luise Kral的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Luise Kral把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Anton能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1959.12** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱)_

- **1960.01** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton和Gustav Winter谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Anton家，钥匙只在白天给。Gustav Winter说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Mitzi Lechner听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Anton把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Anton在院子的水龙头边等到Elisabeth Berg，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Elisabeth Berg拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Anton问要怎样才能收回，Elisabeth Berg说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。

- **1960.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-damaged-deducted` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor
  新住处的门框比旧屋矮，柜子抬进去时顶上的雕花蹭掉一块。Luise 2没当场发作，结账时才从工钱里扣了去，说修补漆得花钱。Maria Huber在旁边直摇头，到底没帮着说话。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (搬家磕坏柜脚被扣工钱)_

  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

  Anton在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Anna 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007848:child_

  一克朗是Anton身上最后一张整票。纸条上的地址在城郊，坐电车又花了几赫勒。工头叼着烟说你来晚了，Anton攥着纸条站了一会儿，回程的路走了一个钟头。
  _effects: cash -1 (职业介绍所登记费（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Anton没去事务所，只在洗衣房打听更便宜的床位。有人提到利奥波德城另一条街上有一间合住的屋子，一个月少一克朗半，只是要跟三个人共用一间。Anton去看了两趟，量了窗子和床的位置，回来把箱子底下的旧报纸铺开算账。
  _effects: flag +looking_for_cheaper_housing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-181_

  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007848:child; food_

  Anton退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，他把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Anton又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

  Anton没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Anton把第三幕撕成四份，塞进铁炉，火苗先舔到的是标题。屋子里有一股纸灰和胶水混在一起的味道。Gustav Lechner在门外敲门催房租，Anton说再等两天，然后坐回桌前，开始写一个只有四页的短篇：一个男人在煤气灯下修一把永远修不好的椅子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:685; skill; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (买新纸和墨水的钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +burned_manuscript_

  Anton把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Anton自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1962.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton没有立刻答话，先去敲Alois Moser的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Alois Moser提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Anton把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002393:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  没有钱请医生，Josefine Hofer把醋瓶拎到床边，拿两只碗轮换着兑水。Hedwig烧得嘴唇起皮，Josefine Hofer用小勺喂了两口温水，又把冷布敷上去。鸡叫时热度退了一点，Hedwig睁开眼，看见Josefine Hofer趴在床沿，手指还攥着湿布。

  铁环在石板缝上颠了一下，Hedwig用钩子一拨，抢先绕过煤车。Mitzi Lechner跟在后面，铁环倒在地上转了两圈。三颗玻璃弹珠从Mitzi Lechner的口袋里摸出来，有一颗还带着体温。Mitzi Lechner把弹珠按进Hedwig手心，转身踢了一脚墙根。

  _effects: condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/truancy-notice` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig在巷口替人跑腿，忘了下午的课。第二天督学站在教室后排，Elisabeth Stein把缺课的名字念出来，Josefine Hofer被叫到学校，罚了两克朗，回家路上没有跟Hedwig说话。那晚Hedwig把跑腿挣的两枚硬币放在桌上，谁也没动。第二天一早自己去了学校。
  _effects: cash -2 (点名册上的第三个空位（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05; flag +truancy_notice; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1919.11** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.win` tags: school, social, childhood
  拼写比赛结束时，Grete Stein敲了敲讲台说“记住这个词”。Hedwig袖口上多了一枚小十字章，回家路上Josef追着要看，最后用三颗李子换了眼福。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; food_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Hedwig前面的大婶一直往后靠，Hedwig就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Hedwig的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1920.05** `vienna-school-childhood.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.spill` tags: school, social, childhood
  锅沿太烫，Hedwig手一松，汤泼在鞋面上。Anton Lechner拽着Hedwig到水龙头下冲脚，Lena蹲在地上刮面包屑。晚饭的汤没了，鞋还湿着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1920.07** `r1-child.epidemic-closure::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/pasteboard-boxes-during-closure` tags: school, epidemic, closure, labour, quarantine, medical, university, knowledge
  停课第三天，Josefine Hofer让Hedwig去印刷所领糊纸盒的活。纸盒堆在床边，Hedwig糊到半夜，手指被浆糊粘得张不开。Emil Lechner来给邻居孩子看病，看见Hedwig在灯下数纸盒，说这孩子该睡觉。
  _effects: cash 2 (停课十四天); condition; flag +child_labour_during_closure; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Hedwig放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Hedwig的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Hedwig也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); role_

- **1921.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Ferdinand Weiss 2让Hedwig把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Hedwig把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Hedwig把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1921.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/traded-for-picture` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  “就一口。”Hedwig把糖块掰开，换了一张火车头。Grete Winter在报亭窗口看着，等糖纸落地，才走出来。画片被扣在柜台上，扫帚递到Hedwig手里。Hedwig扫完半条后巷，Grete Winter用围裙擦了擦画片，还回来时角上多了一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1921.04** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/eraser-cut-in-half` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  教师儿子把Hedwig的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Hedwig没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Hedwig发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Anton Lechner问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-borrowed-from-teacher` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Ferdinand Weiss 2用拇指摸了摸石板上的裂缝，没说话，从讲台抽屉最底下抽出一块旧石板，木框还在，边角被磨得发亮。借的条件是每天放学擦黑板、倒炉灰。Hedwig把石板抱在怀里，当晚就用湿布擦了三遍，第二天袖口全是白粉。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Hedwig被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Hedwig一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Elisabeth Bauer提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: mood -0.05; condition_

- **1922.12** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  Hedwig拼到一半停住，Anton Lechner的尺子落在桌角上。放学后楼梯间只剩Hedwig一个人，把“Notwendigkeit”抄了二十遍，墨水瓶见了底。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Hedwig的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Hedwig在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.foreman-introduction/relative-introduces-trial-half-day` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  亲戚把他带到工头面前，工头让他下星期来试半天，先看裁纸和搬版。母亲熨平的衬衫领子还是硬得硌脖子，他站在厂门口把姓名说了两遍。
  _effects: flag +half_day_trial_at_big_printshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  Maria Kral把案卷合上，问Hedwig知不知道错了。Hedwig的腿在长椅上晃着，够不着地，说“知道”。Josefine Hofer在旁边一直点头，Friedrich Hofer把一张写着“训诫”的纸递给她。出门时，Josefine Hofer把纸折好放进内衣口袋，怕人看见。
  _effects: flag +admonished_by_juvenile_court; role; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the juvenile court keeps a record even without a sentence”_

- **1925.01** `major-education-scholarship-examination/exam-failed` tags: education, scholarship, adolescence, major
  发榜那天，Hedwig在名单上从上往下找了三遍，没有自己的名字。Anton Lechner说作文离题，算术错了一个进位。回家的路上，Josefine Hofer没有提考试，只问晚上想吃什么；Hedwig把借来的课本包好，压在床板底下，第二天照旧去上课。
  _effects: cash -4 (examination fee（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.03; flag +failed_scholarship_exam_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/old-school-withholds-papers` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig等到学期过了一半，转学证还没有下来。原来旧学校不肯放人，说Hedwig欠着图书费。Hedwig把借的书一本本找齐还回去，管理员却找不到借书卡，最后Hedwig自己写了一张收据，按了手印。那张收据按上手印后，管理员才从抽屉里找出借书卡，说早就在这里了。
  _effects: cash -1 (跨区转学的居住证明（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.04999999999999999_

  Friedrich Huber把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Hedwig看期限、工钱和出师考试。Hedwig签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/trial-granted` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Emil Huber没接地址，先看Hedwig的手。纸样堆在墙角，落着灰，Hedwig一张张抖开、按厚薄分摞，纸边把食指划出三道细口。午后Emil Huber端来一碗扁豆汤和半块面包，又把一克朗二十赫勒压在秤砣下，说试工三天，干不满没有工钱。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (试工半日按小时折算的铜币); food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1926.09** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Grete Stein在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Hedwig下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费（其中3.6克朗记入债务）); education secondary; skill_

- **1926.11** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/found-a-loose-bolt` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  检查员来的时候，Maria Lechner让Hedwig和Lena免费坐一趟，说帮忙试试新座椅。座舱升到顶，检查员在下面喊“晃一晃”。Hedwig抓着扶手摇了两下，听见铁架发出一种空心的响声。检查员在本子上写了一个字，Maria Lechner的脸就沉了。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; condition; flag +found_loose_ferris_bolt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; knowledge “one of the ferris wheel bolts is loose”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:scholarship-holder/annual-renewal/revoked` tags: scholarship-holder, education, scholarship, means_tested, state, state_tick, night-school
  Hedwig的奖学金被取消，学校把过去一年的费用开成一张账单。

  Hedwig照着Anton Lechner的笔迹在信尾描了一个签名，又用橡皮把纸边擦毛。信寄出去后，Hedwig每天提早到校，绕着办公室走。一个星期后，Anton Lechner在走廊里叫住Hedwig，只问了一句：我的名字是你写的吗。信纸和蜡封花了一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (推荐信上的第三个名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.05; flag +forged_recommendation_

  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; reputation -0.03; flag +exam_searched_for_notes_

- **1927.08** `r2-leisure.cinema-fire::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::cinema-fire-reform/reported-blocked-fire-exit` tags: leisure, cinema, fire, panic, rescue, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在检查那天看见消防水带被挪去压幕布，检查员一走，又挪了回去。Hedwig把这事告诉Emil Weiss 2，Emil Weiss 2说别多事，老板刚交了罚款。当晚电影放到一半，Hedwig一直盯着侧门，手心里全是汗。
  _effects: mood -0.06; reputation 0.020000000000000004; condition; flag +reported_blocked_fire_exit; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “the manager moved the fire hose back behind the screen”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  Friedrich Winter看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Friedrich Hofer求了半天，Friedrich Winter答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Lena干了三天就走了，Hedwig留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Friedrich Winter才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Hedwig把油印纸折成四折，塞进外套内袋，在工厂换班时一张张递出去。有人接了就走，有人揉成团扔在地上。一个老工人把纸展开，读了两行，塞进帽檐里，说这东西比面包顶饱。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +distributed_political_pamphlet; relation colleague with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; organization_membership; knowledge “workers accept the pamphlets at the factory gate”_

  Katharina Lechner说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Hedwig拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

  补考那天，Hedwig在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Katharina Berg跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Hedwig去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1930.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-farm/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, rural, agriculture
  Alois Winter把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.01** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Luise Kral一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1930.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/mocked-by-younger-students` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig被年轻同学笑说口音重，念课文时全班哄笑。Hedwig把书合上，第二天没来。Grete Stein托人带话，Hedwig只说家里忙，其实每天那个时辰都坐在河边，用树枝在泥地上划自己的名字。河水涨起来的那天，Hedwig在泥地上写的名字被冲掉了，Hedwig又写了一遍。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; skill_

- **1930.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Hermann Moser说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Hermann Moser陪着Hedwig去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1930.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Friedrich Hofer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Alois Huber 2把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Alois Huber 2说不是Hedwig的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Hedwig心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1931.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Luise Berg把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.archive` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Hedwig的信写得好，笔画干净，算盘也快，可速记一栏空了大半。Elisabeth Bauer 4把卷子批了“文书可用，速记欠熟”，人事科把他安排到档案室抄旧保单，日薪两克朗四十赫勒，比正式办事员少一截。桌旁没有窗，只有一盏悬在头顶的灯，他抄到下班时右肩发僵。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (档案室抄写日薪); skill; flag +awaiting_clerk_slot; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Alois Huber 2在楼梯间坐了很久；Hedwig在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Hedwig的名字；此后邻居提起Hedwig，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

- **1932.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Anton在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Anton也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:124; flag +accusation_echoed; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  Lena把Anton的帽子扔进院子，Anton又把晾衣杆撂倒。Hedwig站在两人中间，先把帽子捡起来拍干净，再让两人各说一遍是谁先动的手。说到第三遍，两人都不那么肯定了；Hedwig把帽子放在桌上，说谁再扔就赔谁的工钱。
  _effects: flag +mediated_sibling_fight; role; skill; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Karl Kraus指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1932.05** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Hedwig回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.06000000000000001 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Hermann 2。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Alois Huber 2把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:134_

  Luise Kral把签名纸翻过来，说地址不全，有几页只写了楼号没有门牌。Hedwig说那些人怕报复，Luise Kral说怕报复就别签名。纸被退回来，边角已经卷了。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +petition_rejected_for_missing_addresses; knowledge “the office rejects petitions without a full address for ever”_

- **1933.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Katharina Bauer借给Hedwig一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Hedwig睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.06000000000000005_

  Hedwig和Alois Huber 2在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Alois Huber 2把煤钱推过来，Hedwig接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Luise 2。接生的人收六克朗，Alois Huber 2在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.07999999999999996_

  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Emil Kral排在前面，回头替Hedwig看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Hedwig用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1937.01** `r14-edu.archive-assistant::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/volunteered-in-vain` tags: education, archive_research, archive, research, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Hedwig在档案馆做了一个冬天没有工钱的义工，编完了三箱信札。Friedrich Hofer说经费没有批下来，只能给一张感谢状。Hedwig把感谢状夹进笔记本，第二年没有再出现。地下室的钥匙还留在门房，Friedrich Hofer没有去取。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +volunteered_at_archive; skill_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Ferdinand Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006652_

- **1937.08** `r13-turn.addiction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sought-the-doctor` tags: health, addiction, alcohol, family, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig在上班时手抖得拿不住锉刀，被工头送去门诊。医生说再喝下去肝会坏，给了一张戒酒所的地址。Hedwig去了，门口排队的人比想象中多。轮到时，Hedwig说自己只是睡不着，医生说，那就先从今晚不喝开始。
  _effects: cash -3 (酒馆后门的第二瓶（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +sought_help_for_drinking; role; relation friend with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the clinic offers a supervised withdrawal programme”_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Hedwig在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Anna Huber记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Hedwig带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中2.5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Katharina Kral。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Kral在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:201_

- **1938.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Luise Huber在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Luise Huber也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:202; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Luise Huber病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.18 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006875_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Hedwig能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 22 -> 26; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 16; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_
