  Hedwig在保险公司的窗口拦住Luise Kral，说Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)上个月刚把保额加了一倍，还问过“烧光了能不能赔”。Luise Kral把烟掐了，重新去看仓库的账。三天后，Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)被带走，Helene Weiss在院子里放了一挂小鞭炮，被巡警罚了款。
  _effects: condition; flag +testified_against_landlord_over_fire; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child; knowledge “the landlord doubled the insurance a month before the fire”_

  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Gustav Kral。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Emil Kral把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.05 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:217_

  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 15 -> 7; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -4 (烧黑的房梁); mood -0.07000000000000006; condition; flag +work13_shop_boarded_up_after_fire; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

  Hedwig不想打官司，把工具分成两堆，让Lena先挑。Lena挑走了刨子和凿子，留下那台旧车床；Hedwig把车床擦了一遍，发现底座下面压着父亲刻的一行小字：给肯留下来的那个。Anna Huber在旁边看着，没有说话。
  _effects: cash 2 (封蜡下面的名字); flag +split_the_fathers_tools; role; knowledge “父亲在车床底下刻了一行字：给肯留下来的那个。”_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 2; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_

  Hedwig在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Hedwig去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Hedwig数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

- **1940.01** `kernel-family.inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/inheritance.equal-split` tags: task, task:family, family, inheritance, siblings, estate, law, vienna
  办事处二楼的暖气不热，Emil Berg 2把父亲的存款清单摊在桌上：八十四克朗，扣除丧葬费还剩六十二。Hedwig和Lena一人一半；Lena先把先前从父亲铁盒里拿走的十五克朗放回桌上，余下的十六克朗由办事处补。出门后两人往相反方向走，约好清明一起上坟。
  _effects: estate; cash 16 (remaining share of the father’s savings paid at the office); relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +inheritance_settled; knowledge “父亲的存款共八十四克朗，扣掉丧葬费后一人三十一克朗。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  Hedwig在警察局坐了一夜，问话的人换了三个，问题却只有一个：那天集会上站在第几排。天亮时，Luise Kral让Hedwig签一份“不再参加非法集会”的保证书。Hedwig签了，出门时发现自己的鞋带被剪断了一根，只能拖着走回家。

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Emil Kral在楼梯间坐了很久；Hedwig在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Hedwig的名字；此后邻居提起Hedwig，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。

  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; skill_

- **1941.12** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death

  _effects: death (肺炎)_

- **1916.03** `birth` tags: birth

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000443_

- **1918.01** `state_exit:raising-child` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner不再处于「家里有个正在长大的孩子」；条件已经改变。
  _effects: state_remove raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:18_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Grete Winter把Hermann Lechner拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Hermann Lechner把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Hermann Lechner蹲下把两包并排，按Grete Winter说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Hermann Lechner在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Alois Lechner把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Hermann Lechner额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Hermann Lechner能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Alois Lechner用指尖替Hermann Lechner拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1924.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Friedrich Weiss家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Hermann Lechner递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Friedrich Weiss端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Hermann Lechner把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1924.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Hermann Lechner跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Hermann Lechner从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Josefine Hofer喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Hermann Lechner手心。Hermann Lechner低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Josefine Hofer扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1925.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Hermann Lechner前面的大婶一直往后靠，Hermann Lechner就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Hermann Lechner的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000004 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1925.07** `r14-soc.patron-child::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/borrowed-lessons` tags: cross_class_childhood, childhood_friendship, charity, shame, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Berg在厨房教Hermann Lechner认了半页字母，条件是别让女主人看见。Berta Winter把用剩的练习本塞过来，封面上还印着校徽。Hermann Lechner拿回家，用橡皮擦了三遍，才敢在第一页写下自己的名字。那本练习本后来被翻得掉了封皮，第一页的名字还在，封皮上的校徽被擦得只剩一圈金边。
  _effects: mood 0.08; flag +borrowed_lessons; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000148:child; knowledge “the alphabet came from a rich household cook”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1925.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Grete Stein已经站在旁边。Hermann Lechner的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Grete Stein看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Hermann Lechner盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1926.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work

- **1929.05** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Hermann Lechner在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Hermann Lechner用牙咬断，重新来。Alois Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Hermann Lechner把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1930.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/first-recital` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Lechner在期末演奏会上拉完一首短曲，弓毛断了两根，最后一个音还是稳住了。Katharina Berg在台下点头，师母把手套摘下来鼓掌。回家后，Hermann Lechner把琴擦了三遍，才发现肩托把脖子磨出一道红印，碰一下就疼。Hermann Lechner没有告诉任何人。琴盒没关。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +first_recital; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1931.01** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Lechner的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Anton Hofer在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Hermann Lechner在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.08; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:lc17-child-prodigy/practice-year/keeps-practicing` tags: lc17-child-prodigy, childhood, promise, talent, pressure, family, education, prodigy
  Hermann Lechner今年自己把练习时间从半小时加到三刻钟。琴谱的边角被翻得起毛，厨房里有人听见同一段反复了七遍，最后一遍终于没有错音；Hermann Lechner停下来，才发现窗外天已经黑了，煤气灯在玻璃上照出一小块黄色的自己。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 48 -> 51; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 84 -> 88; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 18 -> 16; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1931.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Lechner的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Anna Huber让Hermann Lechner去区公所补。Hermann Lechner跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Anna Huber看见Hermann Lechner把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Hermann Lechner赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Hermann Lechner第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1931.06** `r2-leisure.cinema-fire::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::cinema-fire-reform/cinema-fire-drill-worked` tags: leisure, cinema, fire, panic, rescue, reform, medical, schooling
  消防检查刚过，电影院换了新的石棉幕布，门口挂着“出口畅通”。放映到一半，胶片又卡了，焦味从放映室飘出来。Hermann Moser第一个冲到门口，把两扇门都推开。Hermann Weiss在里面喊只是卡片，别慌。观众还是走了一半。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; condition; flag +cinema_fire_drill_worked; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; knowledge “the new fire doors open outward and were tested during a jam”_

- **1931.08** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Hermann Lechner在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Katharina Berg跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Hermann Lechner去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; education apprenticeship_

- **1931.10** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Alois Lechner说完就把算术本推到桌角。Hermann Lechner知道再争也没用。Grete Stein在门口拦住Hermann Lechner，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Hermann Lechner把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.learn-instrument::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/self-taught` tags: culture, music, instrument, learning, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Lechner没有钱请老师，就趴在窗边听隔壁的留声机，再把听到的调子按在琴弦上。手指磨出茧子，音还是不准，但他第一次拉完了一整支曲子。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_instrument_by_ear; skill_

- **1932.04** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Katharina Berg把成绩单按在讲台上，说Hermann Lechner还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Hermann Lechner签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1932.05** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/tested-the-new-brake` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Emil Weiss拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Hermann Lechner问这是不是坏了，Emil Weiss说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Anton Winter在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.03; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”_

- **1932.07** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Hermann Lechner站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Katharina Berg追出来说座位可以保留两周，Hermann Lechner点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.04_

- **1933.01** `r3-teen.first-cigarette::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/coughs` tags: smoking, friendship, risk, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Weiss把烟卷塞进Hermann Lechner嘴里，划了根火柴。第一口呛得Hermann Lechner眼泪直流，第二口装作没事。Gustav Weiss笑得蹲在地上，说城里人都这么学。Hermann Lechner把烟夹在指间，直到烧到指节才扔掉。
  _effects: flag +tried_first_cigarette; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000306:child_

- **1933.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/paid-night-class` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Hermann Lechner把六枚克朗按在登记簿旁，Grete Stein抬起眼镜看了看他的出生日期，在夜班名单上写下名字。他领到一张油印课程表，纸角还湿着。
  _effects: cash -6 (职业夜校第一期学费); education apprenticeship; skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1933.06** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Rosa说该自己走，Hermann Lechner说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Hermann Lechner摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: mood -0.07_

- **1933.11** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Huber把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Hermann Lechner看期限、工钱和出师考试。Hermann Lechner签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:137; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag -child_prodigy; education apprenticeship_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1934.01** `r13-edu.dropout-return::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/returned-to-school` tags: education, dropout, return, school, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Lechner退学半年后，在工厂门口看见以前的同学背着书包走过。Hermann Lechner回家翻出课本，书页已经发黄。第二天，Hermann Lechner去找Grete Stein，Grete Stein让Hermann Lechner先跟班旁听两周。Hermann Lechner坐在最后一排，把落下的笔记一页页补回来。两个月后，Hermann Lechner的座位从最后一排换到中间。
  _effects: flag +returned_to_school; education secondary_

- **1934.03** `r1-hc.gang-recruitment::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::gang-crisis-unemployed-brother/gang-crisis-warns-sister` tags: adolescence, gang, recruitment, belonging, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Lechner没有接外套，回家把妹妹的课本从书包里倒出来，说以后别走台球房那条巷。妹妹问为什么，Hermann Lechner说那里有人专门等穿新鞋的孩子；窗外，Elisabeth Winter正靠在墙上抽烟。
  _effects: flag +warned_sibling_about_gang; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000272:child; knowledge “the gang watches children by their shoes”_

- **1934.07** `r3-crime.pickpocket-ring::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::crowded-station/tried-to-leave` tags: crime, youth, police, poverty, gang, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Lechner在最后一刻缩回手，Emil Weiss 2没有骂，只让Maria Winter把Hermann Lechner带到巷子深处。两个人把Hermann Lechner的鞋脱下来，扔进了运河。Emil Weiss 2说“想走可以，先把欠的饭钱还了”，Maria Winter站在旁边，始终没有抬头。
  _effects: mood -0.2; condition; flag +left_pickpocket_ring_under_threat; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation_end acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1934.11** `r3-teen.inherited-tool::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keeps` tags: family, inheritance, craft, grief, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Lechner把父亲留下的刨子交给她，柄上还留着一层汗渍。Hermann Lechner用拇指摸了摸刃口，发现有人替她磨过。第一天做榫头，刨花卷到手背上，她没有停，直到Alois Lechner说可以了。
  _effects: flag +inherited_fathers_plane; role; skill; relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Alois 2这周主动擦了黑板。Hermann Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005610_

- **1935.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hermann Lechner刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Josef Winter一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Josef Winter的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner被叫到学校，老师说Luise 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hermann Lechner没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Grete Stein听完，说音准还行，但声音太薄，撑不起礼拜天。Hermann Lechner问能不能先站在最后一排，Grete Stein说等下次有空位。回家的路上，Hermann Lechner在无人的巷子里把那首歌唱完。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +choir_audition_rejected_

- **1935.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hermann Lechner把通知念给Alois Winter听，Alois Winter摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Hermann Lechner去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; reputation -0.03; mood -0.05999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  Hermann Lechner拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。

  Hermann Lechner把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 5 -> 7; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 53 -> 50; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `r13-civ.report-story::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/story-spiked` tags: knowledge, journalism, investigation, factory, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Winter看完稿子，说证据只有一个人的口供，先压一压。Hermann Lechner问压到什么时候，Helene Winter说等有人愿意署名。稿子被放进抽屉，Hermann Lechner每次经过印刷厂后门，都看见Gustav Weiss在同一个位置抽烟。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +factory_story_spiked; knowledge “the editor will not run the story without a named source”_

- **1936.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Hermann Lechner把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Maria Weiss把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000565:child; mood -0.09000000000000002; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1936.03** `r15-work.factory-hiring::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired` tags: work, factory, employment, industrial, wage, vienna, urban, crisis
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Hermann Lechner的名字写在班次表上。工钱不高，工时很长，但下个星期开始有固定的收入。Hermann Lechner回家时绕路买了一小块肥皂。
  _effects: employment factory worker @ org-winter-print; state_add factory-employment state:world-vienna-1912:174_

- **1936.03** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Hermann Lechner没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 5; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Hermann Lechner当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1937.01** `r15-work.first-commission::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/paid-in-full` tags: work, craft, journeyman, commerce, reputation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Bauer来取货，把工钱一张张数在桌上。Hermann Lechner第一次没有经过师傅的手接过钱。晚上收工后，Hermann Lechner把工具箱擦了一遍，又把订单簿翻到新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (第一笔自己的订单); mood 0.06; reputation 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005840_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Hermann Lechner不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Hermann Lechner拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Hermann Lechner的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/hurt` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner在换班时被机器擦伤了手臂。工头让她去水龙头下面冲一冲，然后回来继续。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter factory-employment.injured false -> true_

- **1937.01** `r13-civ.help-refugee::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sent-to-relief-office` tags: social, refugee, shelter, war, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Lechner没有让Emil Weiss 2进门，只把救济站的地址写在烟盒背面，又画了怎么绕过岗亭。Emil Weiss 2把烟盒攥在手里，鞠了一躬。第二天，走廊的墙上多了一张用铅笔写的谢谢。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +directed_refugee_to_relief; knowledge “the relief office opens at seven and does not ask for a resi”_

- **1937.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Hermann Lechner把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Helene Weiss提着空碗上来时，只看见Hermann Lechner把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food_

- **1937.07** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hermann Lechner觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Friedrich Winter在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1937.11** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Hermann Lechner把照片铁盒交给Luise Huber抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Gustav Berg在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Hermann Lechner排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/held-on` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner又在机器边站满了一年。手指上的茧比去年硬，工钱没有涨。

  Katharina Bauer把招租纸从门上揭下来，领Hermann Lechner看了后间。屋里只有一张铁床和一只裂了口的洗手盆，窗户对着天井。Hermann Lechner付了六克朗，Katharina Bauer把钥匙从围裙里解下来，说晚上十点关大门。
  _effects: cash -6 (新城区门牌下的招租纸（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +lodging_secured_in_new_district; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the front door is locked at ten every night”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1938.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Luise Stein把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Hermann Lechner去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05000000000000002 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  Hermann Lechner只舀出半袋，说自家也不宽裕，要Alois Lechner把上次的煤钱一并记在纸上。Alois Lechner接过纸片，当着面把日期和数目重写一遍，两人各留一半。面粉少了，账却清楚了，Hermann Lechner把纸片夹进账簿样的旧本子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1938.04** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Gustav Weiss把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Hermann Lechner留在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Hermann Lechner在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000306:child; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

  图样在交货前被Gustav Lechner带去另一家作坊。Hermann Lechner在市场上看见同样的书柜，铜边更薄，价格低三成；买主不知道差别，只说这家做得快。Hermann Lechner把尾款收下，回到作坊把原图锁进柜子。

  _effects: cash -8 (后巷仓库的样布（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +work13_bought_supplies_cash_only_

  Hermann Lechner把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +rent_installment_plan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  _effects: cash -20 (催款单贴在门框上（其中18克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +debt_default; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; knowledge “a court summons follows the unpaid debt”_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  四克朗放在桌上，Hermann Lechner数了一遍，又添进房租的信封。孩子半夜翻来覆去，学徒在厨房里咳嗽；Hermann Lechner睁着眼到天亮，眼下青了一圈。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition_

- **1941.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.sanitation-inspection/sign-and-demand-repair` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Emil Weiss 2蹲在下水道口闻了闻，用铅笔在本子上画了个圈。Hermann Lechner指着墙根渗出的黑水，说去年就堵了，看门人只拿木板盖着；Rosa Lechner把湿衣服往旁边挪。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “卫生巡查员把下水道堵塞记进了记录，说会通知房东限期修”_

- **1942.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  疗养院的护士接过行李，把床号写在小黑板上。Hermann Lechner躺下时听见走廊尽头有人在放唱片，咳嗽没那么紧了。安娜的信第三天到，说花浇过了，房东没来催租。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.11999999999999998 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Hermann Lechner守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。

  排在他前面的Rosa Lechner 2很快过了关。轮到他时，医生把灯挪近了些，他还是念错。出了南站，太阳才刚爬上车皮，他站在路边把揉皱的证明捋平。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  Hermann Lechner和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Hermann Lechner在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.020000000000000018; health -0.02 (在湿冷街头耗到傍晚)_

- **1942.09** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  圣文生会的修女在登记簿上写下Hermann Lechner的门牌号，递过面包券和一小袋土豆。Josef在旁边按了指印作保，说这户男人在工厂有活。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hermann Lechner上楼敲了Otto Hofer的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Otto Hofer把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Hermann Lechner能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Hermann Lechner没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008790_

  主顾在契约上按了印，要求一把带黄铜镶边的胡桃木书柜。Hermann Lechner带着Mitzi Moser做了四十天，刨花堆到窗台；交货那天，主顾把尾款数了两遍，又在工匠名册上添了Hermann Lechner的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 师傅作坊 (household_firm); position 师傅 @workshop:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:009311; state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:284; cash 4 (masterwork commission fee); skill; reputation 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; flag +masterwork_accepted; knowledge “主顾把尾款数了两遍，并把我的名字写进了工匠名册。”; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

- **1943.01** `r3-war.armistice-eleven::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fired-last-round` tags: war, armistice, ceasefire, aftermath, memory, vienna, urban, crisis
  停火前十分钟，Elisabeth Moser还让打最后一轮。Hermann Lechner把炮弹推进膛，手却停在拉火绳上；Katharina Weiss说“别打了”，Hermann Lechner说“不打要上军事法庭”。炮响后，对面没有还击，只有一只钢盔从壕边滚下去。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +fired_last_round_before_armistice; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000431:child; knowledge “the last shell was fired minutes before the armistice”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:285; cash 2 (首演酬劳); role; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: position_end 师傅 @workshop:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000443:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:009311; reputation -0.06; mood -0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +carried_out_of_office; knowledge “名牌在会议结束前就被翻了过去。”_

  Friedrich Huber说没有现钱，指了指墙角的缝纫机。Hermann Lechner和Otto Lechner把机器抬上板车，Friedrich Huber的妻子站在楼梯口，手里还攥着没做完的活。机器折了四克朗，剩下的六克朗算清；下楼时Hermann Lechner听见门在背后关上，插销落得很重。
  _effects: cash 4 (门口的三次敲门); food; flag +work13_took_goods_for_debt; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  Lena的信从战地寄来，Anna Stein先拆开读了一遍，再把能念的段落念给Hermann Lechner听。念到“等我”时，Anna Stein停了一下，把信纸折回去，说剩下的等打完仗再读。
  _effects: role; relation partner with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

  Hermann Lechner把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

  Elisabeth Huber 2把搪瓷缸伸过来，说已经两天没找到活。Hermann Lechner把勺子沉到底，捞了稠的给他，后面立刻有人喊不公平。Friedrich Weiss把锅盖一扣，说再吵就都别吃。队伍安静下来，只剩勺子碰缸的声音。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; flag +soup_kitchen_crisis_shift; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

  Franz Winter用了Hermann Lechner的煤，却没有添回去。Hermann Lechner把空煤箱提到她门口，Franz Winter说只借了一铲，等煤车来就还；煤车晚了两天，两家在走廊里碰见都绕着走。煤箱底下还留着上一场雪的湿痕。
  _effects: cash -2 (replacing the coal taken from the box); role; flag +coal_taken_

- **1945.07** `r3-mig.passport-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-passport-exchange/bought-a-place-in-line` tags: migration, papers, queue, bureaucracy, work, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa Lechner在队里替人占位，一个位子六克朗。Hermann Lechner把钱塞过去，下午就站到了窗口前。Elisabeth Moser翻到出生地一栏，说拼写和档案不一致，要回原籍开证明。Rosa Lechner在门外等着分钱，听见这话，先把六克朗往口袋里按了按。
  _effects: cash -6 (雨里的护照队（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +bought_queue_place; relation colleague with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “a queue place costs six crowns”_

  图样在交货前被Gustav Moser带去另一家作坊。Hermann Lechner在市场上看见同样的书柜，铜边更薄，价格低三成；买主不知道差别，只说这家做得快。Hermann Lechner把尾款收下，回到作坊把原图锁进柜子。

  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。

  Hermann Lechner在雪地里捡到一只缝线开裂的钱包，里面有三克朗、一张彩票和一张写着地址的纸。Hermann Lechner按地址送回去，开门的老太太说钱包不是她的，却把彩票塞给Hermann Lechner，说“你拿着吧，我这辈子没中过”。
  _effects: state_add af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:327; flag +returned_the_snow_wallet; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +lucky_idiot; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999982; flag +stopped_a_plainclothes_detective; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; knowledge “the detective was watching the pharmacy for a different case”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002118:child_

- **1947.01** `wc17-sweatshop.the-big-order::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-order-is-taken` tags: sweatshop, ownership, work, factory, order, apprentice, profit, craft
  旅馆老板Emil Kral 2把一张订单摊在刨床上：四十张床架，月底交货。Hermann Lechner算了一遍工时，按现在的做法只能保本；第二天，Hermann Lechner把Ferdinand Bauer的午饭时间缩短，又把工钱改成按件计。Ferdinand Bauer没有反对，只在木料上多画了一道线。
  _effects: state_transition wc17-master-craftsperson -> wc17-sweatshop-owner; cash 4 (hotel order deposit); relation employer with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004054:child; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +took_the_big_order; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “四十张床架的利润来自缩短午饭时间和按件压价。”; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +sweatshop_owner_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Hermann Lechner让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 10 -> 20; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 55 -> 53; cash -2 (worker compensation)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011441_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/hurt` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner在换班时被机器擦伤了手臂。工头让她去水龙头下面冲一冲，然后回来继续。
  _effects: state_parameter factory-employment.injured true -> true_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Hermann Lechner错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 2 -> 1; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 5 -> 15; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙)_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.read-all` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Hermann Lechner把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Hermann Lechner折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

- **1947.08** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Hermann Lechner对Alois Bauer说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Alois Bauer把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Hermann Lechner的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001903:child_

- **1947.10** `r2-faith.elderly-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/waitlisted` tags: charity, elderly, care, family, admission, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Winter在等候名单上写了Alois Lechner的名字，前面还有十九个人。Alois Lechner在门口的长椅上坐了一会儿，说那就先回家。Hermann Lechner把行李箱又提下台阶，箱子比来时重了一点，里面多了一包养老院发的饼干。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +elderly_home_waitlist; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “nineteen people are ahead on the waiting list”_

- **1947.11** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Gustav Hofer怀里的孩子脸烧得通红，Hermann Lechner往后退了半步，把用粉笔写的号码塞进Gustav Hofer手里。药房伙计收了六十赫勒，给一瓶褐色药水；回家路上Hermann Lechner一阵阵发冷。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1948.01** `af17-luck.quiet-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/returned-at-the-box-office` tags: luck, windfall, daily, quiet, money, social, coincidence, vienna
  Hermann Lechner在歌剧院门口的雪水里捡到一只鳄鱼皮钱包，里面有九克朗、一张当晚的站票和一张调琴师的名片。Hermann Lechner把钱包交到售票处，Emil Kral 2赶来时没有数钱，只问Hermann Lechner会不会弹琴。三天后，Emil Kral 2替Hermann Lechner调好了一架旧钢琴，没收工钱，还写了一张去乐器行的介绍信。
  _effects: cash 2 (失主给的谢礼); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; reputation 0.030000000000000027; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 15 -> 5; skill; flag +returned_a_lost_wallet; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Hermann Lechner，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Hermann Lechner说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Hermann Lechner自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 5 -> 17; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011994_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:factory-employment/shift-year/held-on` tags: factory-employment, work, factory, employment, industrial, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner又在机器边站满了一年。手指上的茧比去年硬，工钱没有涨。
  _effects: state_parameter factory-employment.seniority 5 -> 6_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Hermann Lechner在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Hermann Lechner一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 1 -> 2; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 17 -> 23; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/ledger-balances` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  账本合上时，Hermann Lechner又多出三克朗：少算的工时、扣下的学徒午饭、一批没报税的麻布。车间里没人抬头，机器声盖住了算盘声。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 53 -> 58; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 35 -> 33; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 20 -> 22; cash 2 (workshop profit)_

- **1948.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Hermann Lechner把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Luise Lechner的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Hermann Lechner就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Hermann Lechner把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.05999999999999999 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses)_

- **1948.10** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/took-the-room` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Marie Huber趁Hermann Lechner不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Hermann Lechner回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Marie Huber站在门里，说房东已经同意。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000007; relation rival with person-marie-huber; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:363_

- **1948.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hermann Lechner把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Maria Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Hermann Lechner把床单对折，腾出一半，Maria Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002389:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Helene Lechner。Hermann Lechner在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Hermann Lechner从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:375_

- **1949.01** `fm17-seance.parlor-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-name-she-could-not-know` tags: faith, ritual, seance, grief, spiritualism, money, doubt, vienna
  Grete Stein把窗帘拉上，让Hermann Lechner把手放在桌面上。铜盘轻轻响了一声，Grete Stein说出了一个只有Hermann Lechner和死者知道的小名，还提到葬礼那天落在棺盖上的一枚铜纽扣。Hermann Lechner没有哭，只把手指从桌面上收回来，握住了自己的袖口。
  _effects: cash -2 (降神会的场地费与蜡烛钱（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; knowledge “降神会说出了只有我和死者知道的一件小事。”; flag +fm17_seance_comforted; state_add fm17-cult-initiate state:world-vienna-1912:376; flag +fm17_cult_joined_

  又一年过去，Hermann Lechner在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Hermann Lechner一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。

  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Hermann Lechner把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。

- **1949.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Hermann Lechner又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `r13-work.ask-foreman::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/given-broom-and-number` tags: work, hiring, foreman, favour, queue, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Huber正蹲在机器边上换皮带，听完没抬头，只把粉笔从耳朵后面取下来，在门框上写了个“七”。他说七点前来，先扫地、添油，工钱按杂工算。Hermann Lechner把那个数字记在手心，回家路上一直攥着拳头，怕汗把它抹掉。
  _effects: cash 1 (工头兜里的粉笔头); flag +work13_foreman_gave_a_start; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; employment Hilfsarbeiter @ org-winter-print_

  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 22 -> 32; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 58 -> 56; cash -2 (worker compensation（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1949.12** `r1-civ.requisition-cart::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cart-and-horse-requisitioned` tags: war, requisition, property, army, shortage, vienna, urban, postwar
  军队征收站设在市场边上，Anna Huber把一张征收单拍在Hermann Lechner的车上，说要征用马和车，按市价给收据。Hermann Lechner问收据什么时候能兑，Anna Huber说“战后”。Klara在旁边牵着自己的驴，缰绳攥得发白。
  _effects: cash -8 (征收单上的马（其中8克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +cart_and_horse_requisitioned; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation colleague with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the receipt is redeemable after the war, without interest”_

  Luise Lechner放学后留在车间里数扣子，一只手指被针扎穿。Hermann Lechner抱起Luise Lechner往诊所跑，路上想起自己十二岁那年也在同一排机器前站过；第二天，Hermann Lechner把Luise Lechner送回了学校。
  _effects: cash -2 (clinic fees for the child（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 32 -> 47; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 33 -> 28; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 53 -> 51; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +child_injured; knowledge “孩子的手指被针扎穿，我十二岁时也站在同一排机器前。”_

  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.doubt 25 -> 33; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.belonging 55 -> 57; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/overpraise` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.04** `r13-turn.recovery::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::reform-public-clinic/fever-returned` tags: health, recovery, medicine, body, hope, vienna, urban, postwar
  绷带拆到第三层，伤口又肿了起来。Gustav Hofer说是里面还有碎布，要再清一次。Hermann Lechner问能不能不住院，Gustav Hofer说不住院就得每天来换药。Hermann Lechner算了算车钱和换药钱，最后选择隔天来，结果伤口拖了两个月才收口。
  _effects: cash -8 (拆掉绷带的那天（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +recovery_complicated; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1951.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Hermann Lechner一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Otto Huber送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  _effects: state_remove af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:327; flag -lucky_idiot_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/ledger-balances` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  账本合上时，Hermann Lechner又多出三克朗：少算的工时、扣下的学徒午饭、一批没报税的麻布。车间里没人抬头，机器声盖住了算盘声。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 46 -> 51; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 28 -> 26; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 57 -> 59; cash 2 (workshop profit)_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:fm17-cult-initiate/the-circle-year/keeps-asking` tags: fm17-cult-initiate, faith, cult, belonging, obedience, doubt, exit, religion
  又过了一年，Hermann Lechner仍坐在那间借来的后厅里，但开始记下每次募捐的数目。会长说怀疑也是修行的一部分；Hermann Lechner把这句话抄在本子上，旁边画了一个问号。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.doubt 33 -> 41; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.belonging 57 -> 59; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `state_expire:grief` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick, state:grief
  Hermann Lechner的「丧亲之痛」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove grief state:world-vienna-1912:398_

- **1951.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/served-three-hours` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Lechner站在汤锅后面，把勺子往碗沿上磕两下，再递给下一只手。队伍从门口拐到巷子里，有人端着碗蹲在墙根吃。收摊时，Elisabeth Moser把锅底最后一点汤刮给Hermann Lechner，说今天够了。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +volunteered_at_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1951.04** `p9-work-transport.foreman-favor/foreman-favor-ask` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, overtime, foreman
  Hermann Lechner在工间休息时走到Katharina Stein 3面前，问加班到底按什么排。工头头也没抬，说名单是车间主任批的，就这八个人，嫌多事可以去隔壁车间问问。名单最终没改，Hermann Lechner回到机床边，Grete Weiss递过来一支烟，也没说话。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004093:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1951.04** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Hermann Lechner让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 47 -> 57; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 48 -> 46; cash -2 (worker compensation（其中2克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013727_

- **1951.05** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Hermann Lechner把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Hermann Lechner的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

- **1951.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Hermann Lechner站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001903:child; skill_

- **1952.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/wrong-register` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, postwar
  登记处的老职员把Anton Lechner的名字写成了Hermann Lechner小时候的名字。Hermann Lechner指出错误，对方却说“你们家的人长得都一样”；Anton Lechner拿着写错名字的课程表回家，一路上都在偷笑。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 80 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 25 -> 58; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 2; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; flag +wrong_school_register_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/strike-at-the-gate` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  车间里有人吹了一声口哨，机器一台接一台停下来。Hermann Lechner站在办公室门口，先看见的是订单，后看见的是几十张没有表情的脸。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 26 -> 18; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 51 -> 48; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 75 -> 85_

  会长让Hermann Lechner在一张空白纸上签名，说这是“把旧名字交出去”。签完，Hermann Lechner才发现那页纸被夹进了会员名册；这个月的奉献又涨了一克朗，没有人解释为什么。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 50 -> 58; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 35 -> 41; cash -1 (上涨的月度奉献)_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.interview-worker::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/foreman-interrupts` tags: knowledge, interview, worker, trust, journalism, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Lechner刚把本子掏出来，Ferdinand Hofer 3就从后面走过来，问是不是在记工时。Luise 2立刻改口，说在借火柴。Ferdinand Hofer 3看着Hermann Lechner把本子合上，说车间不是采访的地方。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; flag +interview_interrupted_by_foreman; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-159_

- **1952.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Katharina Winter把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Hermann Lechner去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001430:child_

- **1952.07** `fm17-cult.exit-cost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/stays-for-the-child` tags: faith, cult, exit, family, child, money, belonging, vienna
  Hermann Lechner已经把行李收到门口，Josef Lechner却从院里跑回来，说学堂里有人答应教他写字，还说外面的孩子会笑他没有父亲。Hermann Lechner在门槛上坐了很久，最后把行李又提回屋里。第二天，Hermann Lechner照常去后厅摆椅子。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 40 -> 50; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 20 -> 35; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.doubt 41 -> 51; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +fm17_cult_stayed_for_child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1952.12** `wc17-factory.accident::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hand-in-the-press` tags: factory, accident, injury, work, safety, wage, vienna, urban
  皮带在换班时打滑，Hermann Lechner的右手被卷进压机的辊子。Elisabeth Bauer 3让人去叫厂医，自己先关掉机器；工友们围过来时，Hermann Lechner看见自己的血滴在还没裁完的军服布上。
  _effects: cash -2 (medical fees and lost wages（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter factory-employment.injured true -> true; flag +factory_accident; knowledge “压机的皮带在换班时打滑，护罩没有扣紧。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  主顾在交货前改了主意：城里时兴更轻的样式，胡桃木太沉。Hermann Lechner把做好的柜门卸下来，木料退不掉，只能抵给料行。Emil Winter问还做不做，Hermann Lechner说先把欠账还清。
  _effects: cash -2 (materials that could not be returned（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001470:child; flag +commission_lost; knowledge “胡桃木柜门已经做好，主顾却改要轻样式，木料退不回料行。”_

  Hermann Lechner拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Hermann Winter把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Hermann Lechner把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; organization_funds_

- **1953.02** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lie-that-stuck` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Moser告诉所有人Hermann Lechner偷了东西。没有人找到赃物，但每个人都在传。Hermann Lechner去市场时，摊主把零钱放在柜台上，不再递到手里。
  _effects: reputation -0.1; flag +false_accusation; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003953:child; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:466_

- **1953.02** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, betrayal, family
  Hermann Lechner走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: reputation -0.03_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014946_

- **1953.05** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hermann Lechner在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Otto Berg却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Hermann Lechner把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1953.09** `r2-law.legal-aid::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r2-law.legal-aid-reform/municipal-clinic` tags: law, legal_aid, poverty, court, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, postwar
  市里新设了法律咨询处，每周三下午开门。Hermann Lechner去时门口已经排了二十多人，Gustav Hofer一次只能谈十五分钟。轮到Hermann Lechner时，Berta Moser敲了敲钟，说时间到；Gustav Hofer把案卷塞进包里，让Hermann Lechner下周三再来，带上所有欠条。
  _effects: flag +used_municipal_legal_clinic; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; knowledge “the municipal clinic allows fifteen minutes per case”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1954.01** `af17-humiliation.puppet-show::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/laughed-first` tags: humiliation, public, scandal, theatre, charity, reputation, absurd, vienna
  慈善市集的木偶戏把Hermann Lechner的丑闻编成三幕，木偶穿着和Hermann Lechner一样的灰外套，连走路的姿势都学得出来。第一幕还没演完，Hermann Lechner先笑出了声，笑得比谁都响；人群愣了一下，也跟着笑。市长夫人当场请Hermann Lechner为下一场义卖剪彩，Elisabeth Moser在幕布后面朝Hermann Lechner举了举木偶。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; cash 2 (下一场义卖的剪彩酬劳); state_remove public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:466; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; flag +laughed_at_own_caricature; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Hermann Lechner把第四十个榫头敲进卯眼，声音是闷的，不晃。Gustav Hofer路过，用拇指按了按接缝，说这回不用胶也能站住。Hermann Lechner把废掉的三十九个榫头扫进灶膛，火苗从锯末里窜起来，屋里全是松木的味。

  Hermann Lechner排在圣文生会侧门的队伍里，前面的人领走最后一条咸鱼。轮到时修女从箱底翻出一袋土豆，Katharina Huber 2帮着拎到街角。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.06 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

  Hermann Lechner把Elisabeth Huber 2和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Hermann Lechner把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。

  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中0.25克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

  _effects: cash 2 (把铺盖搬上三楼); role; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000148:child; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006440:child; household; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006440:child_

  _effects: cash 10 (友人借给搬家应急的十克朗); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Hermann Lechner正要把空床许给Mitzi Lechner 2，Friedrich Lechner 2却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Hermann Lechner回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Mitzi Lechner 2，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。

  货送回来时，Hermann Lechner一眼就看出袖口歪了。拆开一看，里面的衬布是旧料，洗过两次就会缩。Anton Winter 4说价钱只够这个做法，Hermann Lechner把整批货退回去，赔了主顾六克朗。Luise Lechner连夜拆线，拆到手指起泡，废布料装了满满一筐。
  _effects: cash -6 (转包给后街的作坊); reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +work13_subcontract_rejected; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147_

  Anton Winter在清单背面发现一行铅笔字：“西屋的柜子归老二”。柜子里有一叠战争债券，Hermann Lechner说忘了报。Anton Winter把债券按面值七折计入遗产，又加了一笔滞纳金；Mitzi在门口听见，问那柜子到底算谁的。Gustav Winter把清单翻过来，说铅笔字不算遗嘱。
  _effects: cash -18 (遗产清单上的犁（其中18克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +war_bonds_found_in_unlisted_cabinet; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the cabinet note is not a valid testamentary disposition”_

  Hermann Lechner在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的走廊里拦住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，话还没说完，对方就摆手说账上没这笔规矩。Hermann Lechner回车间时，工头正往布告栏上钉新的计件单。

- **1960.08** `vienna-postwar-supermarket/stall-closes` tags: adulthood, postwar, consumer, food
  常去买菜的摊子关了，摊主说租金涨得比他卖土豆赚得快。Hermann Lechner改去新开的自助商店，货更整齐，却没人再替他挑最熟的那颗西红柿。
  _effects: food; cash -4 (packaged vegetables（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +market_stall_closed_

- **1960.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hermann Lechner没有立刻答话，先去敲Maria Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Maria Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hermann Lechner把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002389:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  Hermann Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang附近出了事故。工友和路人把人抬到门口，等医生赶到时已经没有了呼吸。Franz Winter在场。
